`port/ghidra/GroundTruth.java`:

```java
import ghidra.app.script.GhidraScript;
import ghidra.program.model.address.Address;
import ghidra.program.model.listing.Function;
import ghidra.program.model.listing.FunctionManager;
import ghidra.program.model.symbol.SourceType;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileReader;
import java.io.PrintWriter;

/**
 * Seed a fresh analysis with the ground truth SRW already figured out.
 * Reads H:/ghidra/reference/SR/SRW-games/Septerra Core/SRW/x86/*.sci and applies:
 *
 *   global_aliases.sci        loc_ADDR,Name   -> rename label/function
 *   external_procedures.sci   loc_ADDR,Name   -> name cross-boundary function
 *   noret_procedures.sci      loc_ADDR        -> mark no-return
 *   fixup_interpret_as_code.sci loc_ADDR      -> disassemble + create function
 *   ignored_areas.sci         loc_ADDR,len    -> clear (data, not code)
 *
 * Then writes function_inventory_labeled.csv.
 */
public class GroundTruth extends GhidraScript {

    private static final String SCI = "H:/ghidra/reference/SR/SRW-games/Septerra Core/SRW/x86/";

    private Address addr(String s) {
        String h = s.trim();
        if (h.startsWith("loc_")) h = h.substring(4);
        return currentProgram.getAddressFactory().getDefaultAddressSpace().getAddress(Long.parseLong(h, 16));
    }

    private void rename(Address a, String name, int[] stats) {
        try {
            FunctionManager fm = currentProgram.getFunctionManager();
            Function f = fm.getFunctionAt(a);
            if (f == null) {
                f = createFunction(a, name);
                if (f != null) { stats[1]++; return; }
                createLabel(a, name, true);
                stats[2]++;
                return;
            }
            f.setName(name, SourceType.IMPORTED);
            stats[0]++;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            stats[3]++;
            println("  rename failed at " + a + " -> " + name + ": " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void run() throws Exception {
        int[] stats = new int[4]; // renamed, created, labelled, failed

        // --- global aliases + external procedures: names ---
        for (String file : new String[]{"global_aliases.sci", "external_procedures.sci"}) {
            File f = new File(SCI + file);
            if (!f.exists()) { println("missing " + file); continue; }
            int n = 0;
            try (BufferedReader br = new BufferedReader(new FileReader(f))) {
                String line;
                while ((line = br.readLine()) != null) {
                    line = line.trim();
                    if (line.isEmpty() || line.startsWith("#")) continue;
                    String[] p = line.split(",");
                    if (p.length < 2) continue;
                    rename(addr(p[0]), p[1].trim(), stats);
                    n++;
                }
            }
            println(file + ": " + n + " entries");
        }

        // --- no-return ---
        File noret = new File(SCI + "noret_procedures.sci");
        if (noret.exists()) {
            int n = 0;
            try (BufferedReader br = new BufferedReader(new FileReader(noret))) {
                String line;
                while ((line = br.readLine()) != null) {
                    line = line.trim();
                    if (line.isEmpty()) continue;
                    Function f = currentProgram.getFunctionManager().getFunctionAt(addr(line));
                    if (f != null) { f.setNoReturn(true); n++; }
                }
            }
            println("noret_procedures.sci: " + n + " marked");
        }

        // --- code ranges the analyzer may have missed ---
        File code = new File(SCI + "fixup_interpret_as_code.sci");
        if (code.exists()) {
            int n = 0;
            try (BufferedReader br = new BufferedReader(new FileReader(code))) {
                String line;
                while ((line = br.readLine()) != null) {
                    line = line.trim();
                    if (line.isEmpty()) continue;
                    Address a = addr(line);
                    if (getInstructionAt(a) == null) { disassemble(a); n++; }
                    if (currentProgram.getFunctionManager().getFunctionAt(a) == null) createFunction(a, null);
                }
            }
            println("fixup_interpret_as_code.sci: " + n + " disassembled");
        }

        // --- data ranges ---
        File ignored = new File(SCI + "ignored_areas.sci");
        if (ignored.exists()) {
            int n = 0;
            try (BufferedReader br = new BufferedReader(new FileReader(ignored))) {
                String line;
                while ((line = br.readLine()) != null) {
                    line = line.trim();
                    if (line.isEmpty() || line.startsWith("#")) continue;
                    String[] p = line.split(",");
                    if (p.length < 2) continue;
                    Address start = addr(p[0]);
                    long len = Long.parseLong(p[1].trim());
                    clearListing(start, start.add(len - 1));
                    n++;
                }
            }
            println("ignored_areas.sci: " + n + " ranges cleared");
        }

        println("GROUNDTRUTH renamed=" + stats[0] + " created=" + stats[1] +
                " labelled=" + stats[2] + " failed=" + stats[3]);

        // --- labeled inventory ---
        File dir = new File("H:/ghidra/work/ghidra-out");
        dir.mkdirs();
        File out = new File(dir, "function_inventory_labeled.csv");
        FunctionManager fm = currentProgram.getFunctionManager();
        int count = 0;
        long bytes = 0;
        try (PrintWriter pw = new PrintWriter(out, "UTF-8")) {
            pw.println("address,name,bytes,params,thunk,noReturn");
            for (Function f : fm.getFunctions(true)) {
                pw.println(f.getEntryPoint() + "," + f.getName() + "," +
                        f.getBody().getNumAddresses() + "," + f.getParameterCount() + "," +
                        f.isThunk() + "," + f.hasNoReturn());
                count++;
                bytes += f.getBody().getNumAddresses();
            }
        }
        println("LABELED_INVENTORY functions=" + count + " bytes=" + bytes + " file=" + out.getAbsolutePath());
    }
}

`port/ghidra/Inventory.java`:

```java
import ghidra.app.script.GhidraScript;
import ghidra.program.model.listing.Function;
import ghidra.program.model.listing.FunctionManager;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.PrintWriter;

/**
 * Dump every recovered function to CSV so the decompilation effort can be sized.
 * Run as a -postScript after analysis.
 */
public class Inventory extends GhidraScript {
    @Override
    public void run() throws Exception {
        File dir = new File("H:/ghidra/work/ghidra-out");
        dir.mkdirs();
        File out = new File(dir, "function_inventory.csv");

        FunctionManager fm = currentProgram.getFunctionManager();
        int n = 0;
        long totalBytes = 0;
        int thunks = 0, noret = 0, external = 0;

        try (PrintWriter pw = new PrintWriter(out, "UTF-8")) {
            pw.println("address,name,bytes,params,thunk,noReturn,external");
            for (Function f : fm.getFunctions(true)) {
                long sz = f.getBody().getNumAddresses();
                pw.println(f.getEntryPoint() + "," + f.getName() + "," + sz + "," +
                        f.getParameterCount() + "," + f.isThunk() + "," +
                        f.hasNoReturn() + "," + f.isExternal());
                n++;
                totalBytes += sz;
                if (f.isThunk()) thunks++;
                if (f.hasNoReturn()) noret++;
                if (f.isExternal()) external++;
            }
        }
        println("INVENTORY functions=" + n + " bytes=" + totalBytes +
                " thunks=" + thunks + " noreturn=" + noret + " external=" + external);
        println("INVENTORY file=" + out.getAbsolutePath());
    }
}

`port/ghidra/RetypeSwitch.java`:

```java
import ghidra.app.decompiler.DecompInterface;
import ghidra.app.decompiler.DecompileResults;
import ghidra.app.script.GhidraScript;
import ghidra.program.model.address.Address;
import ghidra.program.model.data.DataType;
import ghidra.program.model.data.DataTypeManager;
import ghidra.program.model.data.IntegerDataType;
import ghidra.program.model.listing.Function;
import ghidra.program.model.pcode.HighFunction;
import ghidra.program.model.pcode.HighFunctionDBUtil;
import ghidra.program.model.pcode.HighSymbol;
import ghidra.program.model.symbol.SourceType;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.PrintWriter;
import java.util.Iterator;

/**
 * Retype a decompiler local variable (the switch operand) and re-decompile.
 *
 * Ghidra types FUN_00430500's switch operand as `int *` because it is derived from a global
 * by pointer arithmetic, which makes every case render as `case (int *)0x3:`. The global's
 * type cannot fix that -- only the variable's type can.
 *
 * Usage:
 *   -postScript RetypeSwitch.java 0x430500                  (list locals + types)
 *   -postScript RetypeSwitch.java 0x430500 piVar9 GameState (retype, then re-decompile)
 */
public class RetypeSwitch extends GhidraScript {

    private static final String OUT = "H:/ghidra/work/ghidra-out/";

    private DecompileResults decompile(Function f) {
        DecompInterface di = new DecompInterface();
        di.toggleCCode(true);
        di.toggleSyntaxTree(true);
        di.openProgram(currentProgram);
        return di.decompileFunction(f, 300, monitor);
    }

    @Override
    public void run() throws Exception {
        String[] args = getScriptArgs();
        long addr = Long.parseLong((args.length > 0 ? args[0] : "0x430500").replace("0x", ""), 16);
        Address a = currentProgram.getAddressFactory().getDefaultAddressSpace().getAddress(addr);
        Function f = currentProgram.getFunctionManager().getFunctionAt(a);
        if (f == null) { println("ERROR: no function at " + a); return; }

        DecompileResults res = decompile(f);
        if (!res.decompileCompleted()) { println("DECOMP FAILED: " + res.getErrorMessage()); return; }
        HighFunction hf = res.getHighFunction();

        // --- list locals ------------------------------------------------------
        println("LOCALS of " + f.getName() + " (" + a + "):");
        Iterator<HighSymbol> it = hf.getLocalSymbolMap().getSymbols();
        java.util.List<HighSymbol> all = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
        while (it.hasNext()) {
            HighSymbol s = it.next();
            all.add(s);
            DataType dt = s.getDataType();
            println("  " + s.getName() + " : " + (dt == null ? "?" : dt.getName()) +
                    " (size " + (dt == null ? -1 : dt.getLength()) + ", storage " + s.getStorage() + ")");
        }
        println("LOCALS count=" + all.size());

        if (args.length < 3) {
            println("LIST_ONLY (pass <var> <typeName> to retype)");
            return;
        }

        // --- retype -----------------------------------------------------------
        String varName = args[1];
        String typeName = args[2];
        DataTypeManager dtm = currentProgram.getDataTypeManager();
        DataType newType;
        if (typeName.equalsIgnoreCase("int")) {
            newType = IntegerDataType.dataType;
        } else if (typeName.equalsIgnoreCase("int*") || typeName.equalsIgnoreCase("int *")) {
            newType = new ghidra.program.model.data.PointerDataType(IntegerDataType.dataType);
        } else {
            newType = dtm.getDataType("/" + typeName);
            if (newType == null) newType = dtm.getDataType(typeName);
            if (newType == null) { println("ERROR: unknown type " + typeName); return; }
        }
        println("retyping " + varName + " -> " + newType.getName());

        boolean done = false;
        for (HighSymbol s : all) {
            if (!s.getName().equals(varName)) continue;
            try {
                HighFunctionDBUtil.updateDBVariable(s, s.getName(), newType, SourceType.USER_DEFINED);
                println("  retyped " + varName + " (was " + s.getDataType().getName() + ")");
                done = true;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                println("  retype FAILED: " + e.getMessage());
            }
        }
        if (!done) { println("ERROR: variable " + varName + " not found"); return; }

        // --- re-decompile with the new type -----------------------------------
        DisassembleResult2(f);
    }

    private void DisassembleResult2(Function f) throws Exception {
        DecompileResults res2 = decompile(f);
        if (!res2.decompileCompleted()) { println("RE-DECOMP FAILED: " + res2.getErrorMessage()); return; }
        String c = res2.getDecompiledFunction().getC();
        File out = new File(OUT + "decomp_" + f.getEntryPoint() + "_retyped.c");
        try (PrintWriter pw = new PrintWriter(out, "UTF-8")) { pw.print(c); }

        int cases = 0, i = 0;
        while ((i = c.indexOf("case ", i)) >= 0) { cases++; i += 5; }
        int ptrCases = 0; i = 0;
        while ((i = c.indexOf("case (int *)", i)) >= 0) { ptrCases++; i += 12; }

        println("RETYPED_DECOMP lines=" + c.split("\n").length + " bytes=" + c.length() +
                " cases=" + cases + " pointer_cases=" + ptrCases);
        println("RETYPED_DECOMP file=" + out.getAbsolutePath());
        println("RETYPED_DECOMP in_db=" + currentProgram.getFunctionManager().getFunctionAt(f.getEntryPoint()).getName());
    }
}

```

`port/ghidra/RetypeVars.java`:

```java
import ghidra.app.decompiler.DecompInterface;
import ghidra.app.decompiler.DecompileResults;
import ghidra.app.script.GhidraScript;
import ghidra.program.model.address.Address;
import ghidra.program.model.data.DataType;
import ghidra.program.model.data.DataTypeManager;
import ghidra.program.model.data.PointerDataType;
import ghidra.program.model.listing.Function;
import ghidra.program.model.pcode.HighFunction;
import ghidra.program.model.pcode.HighFunctionDBUtil;
import ghidra.program.model.pcode.HighSymbol;
import ghidra.program.model.symbol.SourceType;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.PrintWriter;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Retype decompiler variables of one function, then re-decompile.
 *
 * Ghidra types the object pointer inside ActivateObject as `int` because the value arrives in
 * EAX from a __fastcall caller, so every field access renders as *(uint *)(iVar1 + 0x132)
 * instead of pObj->flags132. The global's type cannot fix that -- only the variable's type can.
 *
 * Usage: -postScript RetypeVars.java 0x428ec0 in_EAX=ObjSlot* iVar1=Obj*
 *        (no assignments -> just list the variables and their current types)
 */
public class RetypeVars extends GhidraScript {

    private static final String OUT = "H:/ghidra/work/ghidra-out/";

    private DecompileResults decompile(Function f) {
        DecompInterface di = new DecompInterface();
        di.toggleCCode(true);
        di.toggleSyntaxTree(true);
        di.openProgram(currentProgram);
        return di.decompileFunction(f, 180, monitor);
    }

    @Override
    public void run() throws Exception {
        List<String> args = new ArrayList<>();
        for (String a : getScriptArgs()) for (String p : a.split(",")) if (!p.trim().isEmpty()) args.add(p.trim());
        if (args.isEmpty()) { println("ERROR: need an address"); return; }

        long addr = Long.parseLong(args.get(0).replace("0x", ""), 16);
        Address a = currentProgram.getAddressFactory().getDefaultAddressSpace().getAddress(addr);
        Function f = currentProgram.getFunctionManager().getFunctionAt(a);
        if (f == null) { println("ERROR: no function at " + a); return; }

        DecompileResults res = decompile(f);
        if (!res.decompileCompleted()) { println("DECOMP FAILED: " + res.getErrorMessage()); return; }
        HighFunction hf = res.getHighFunction();

        List<HighSymbol> syms = new ArrayList<>();
        Iterator<HighSymbol> it = hf.getLocalSymbolMap().getSymbols();
        while (it.hasNext()) syms.add(it.next());

        if (args.size() == 1) {
            println("VARIABLES of " + f.getName() + ":");
            for (HighSymbol s : syms)
                println("  " + s.getName() + " : " + s.getDataType().getName());
            return;
        }

        DataTypeManager dtm = currentProgram.getDataTypeManager();
        for (int i = 1; i < args.size(); i++) {
            String[] kv = args.get(i).split(":");   // Ghidra splits script args on '='
            if (kv.length != 2) { println("  bad assignment: " + args.get(i)); continue; }
            String varName = kv[0].trim(), typeName = kv[1].trim();
            DataType dt;
            if (typeName.endsWith("*")) {
                DataType base = dtm.getDataType("/" + typeName.substring(0, typeName.length() - 1).trim());
                if (base == null) { println("  unknown base type in " + typeName); continue; }
                dt = new PointerDataType(base);
            } else {
                dt = dtm.getDataType("/" + typeName);
                if (dt == null) { println("  unknown type " + typeName); continue; }
            }
            boolean done = false;
            for (HighSymbol s : syms) {
                if (!s.getName().equals(varName)) continue;
                HighFunctionDBUtil.updateDBVariable(s, s.getName(), dt, SourceType.USER_DEFINED);
                println("  retyped " + varName + " -> " + dt.getName());
                done = true;
            }
            if (!done) println("  variable not found: " + varName);
        }

        DecompileResults res2 = decompile(f);
        if (!res2.decompileCompleted()) { println("RE-DECOMP FAILED: " + res2.getErrorMessage()); return; }
        String c = res2.getDecompiledFunction().getC();
        File out = new File(OUT + "decomp_" + Long.toHexString(addr) + "_retyped.c");
        try (PrintWriter pw = new PrintWriter(out, "UTF-8")) { pw.print(c); }
        println("RETYPED_DECOMP lines=" + c.split("\n").length + " file=" + out.getAbsolutePath());
    }
}

```

`port/ghidra/Structs.java`:

```java
import ghidra.app.script.GhidraScript;
import ghidra.program.model.address.Address;
import ghidra.program.model.data.ArrayDataType;
import ghidra.program.model.data.DataType;
import ghidra.program.model.data.DataTypeComponent;
import ghidra.program.model.data.DataTypeConflictHandler;
import ghidra.program.model.data.DataTypeManager;
import ghidra.program.model.data.IntegerDataType;
import ghidra.program.model.data.PointerDataType;
import ghidra.program.model.data.StructureDataType;
import ghidra.program.model.data.ShortDataType;
import ghidra.program.model.data.Undefined1DataType;
import ghidra.program.model.data.UnsignedShortDataType;
import ghidra.program.model.data.VoidDataType;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.PrintWriter;

/**
 * Define the engine's object slot as real struct types and apply them to .bss.
 *
 * Layout proven from the disassembly of BuildObjTables (0x428390):
 *   mov $0x4bf048,%esi / lea -0x8(%esi),%ebx   -> slot base 0x4bf040
 *   push $0xbe8 / call memset                   -> slot size 0xBE8
 *   mov %eax,(%ebx)                             -> slot[0] = object pointer
 *   movl $0xffffffff,0xb08(%esi)                -> slot+0xB10 = -1
 *   mov %eax,0xbc4(%esi) / 0xbc8(%esi)          -> slot+0xBCC, slot+0xBD0 = 1
 *   add $0xbe8,%esi                             -> stride 0xBE8
 *
 * The five 0x1C8 sub-structs come from StartSlotEffects (0x4299c0), which calls
 * StartAnimation at slot+0x228/0x3f0/0x5b8/0x780/0x948 -- spacing exactly 0x1C8 --
 * and switches on the selector at slot+0xB10.
 *
 * Usage: -postScript Structs.java
 */
public class Structs extends GhidraScript {

    private static final String OUT = "H:/ghidra/work/ghidra-out/";
    private DataTypeManager dtm;

    private void put(StructureDataType st, int off, DataType dt, int len, String name, String cmt) {
        try {
            st.replaceAtOffset(off, dt, len, name, cmt);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            println("  WARN replaceAtOffset(" + Integer.toHexString(off) + ") " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private StructureDataType buildEffect() {
        StructureDataType e = new StructureDataType("Effect", 0x1C8, dtm);
        put(e, 0x040, new PointerDataType(VoidDataType.dataType), 4, "animRoot", "resource/animation root");
        put(e, 0x044, UnsignedShortDataType.dataType, 2, "f044", null);
        put(e, 0x05c, IntegerDataType.dataType, 4, "state", "StartSlotEffects writes 2");
        put(e, 0x060, IntegerDataType.dataType, 4, "f060", "StartSlotEffects writes 2");
        put(e, 0x06c, IntegerDataType.dataType, 4, "f06c", "StartSlotEffects writes 1");
        put(e, 0x190, UnsignedShortDataType.dataType, 2, "frameIndex", "index into 0x230-byte frame table");
        put(e, 0x194, new PointerDataType(VoidDataType.dataType), 4, "animList", "StartAnimation appends here");
        put(e, 0x19c, Undefined1DataType.dataType, 1, "loopFlag", "StartAnimation picks flag 1 when 0");
        put(e, 0x1a4, new PointerDataType(VoidDataType.dataType), 4, "f1a4", "bytes +0xe/+0xf copied into obj");
        return e;
    }

    private StructureDataType buildSlot(DataType effect) {
        StructureDataType s = new StructureDataType("ObjSlot", 0xBE8, dtm);
        put(s, 0x000, new PointerDataType(VoidDataType.dataType), 4, "obj", "object pointer");
        put(s, 0x004, IntegerDataType.dataType, 4, "animRoot", "copy of obj->0x164");
        put(s, 0x008, IntegerDataType.dataType, 4, "f008", "*(obj->0x164 + 8)");
        put(s, 0x228, new ArrayDataType(effect, 5, effect.getLength()), 5 * 0x1C8, "effects",
                "five 0x1C8 effect structs; 0x228 + 5*0x1C8 = 0xB10");
        put(s, 0xB10, IntegerDataType.dataType, 4, "activeEffect", "-1 = none; indexes effects[]");
        put(s, 0xBCC, IntegerDataType.dataType, 4, "fBCC", "initialised to 1");
        put(s, 0xBD0, IntegerDataType.dataType, 4, "fBD0", "initialised to 1");
        return s;
    }

    /** The object itself: same animatable header as Effect, plus the object-only fields. */
    private StructureDataType buildObj() {
        StructureDataType o = new StructureDataType("Obj", 0x1C8, dtm);
        put(o, 0x018, Undefined1DataType.dataType, 1, "flags0",
                "byte flags: ActivateObject ors 8 and clears 0x1001; SUB_9 tests bit 1");
        put(o, 0x040, new PointerDataType(VoidDataType.dataType), 4, "animRoot", "same offset as Effect::animRoot");
        put(o, 0x044, UnsignedShortDataType.dataType, 2, "f044", null);
        put(o, 0x05c, IntegerDataType.dataType, 4, "state", "== 4 branches in SUB_9");
        put(o, 0x06c, IntegerDataType.dataType, 4, "f06c", null);
        put(o, 0x0a2, ShortDataType.dataType, 2, "f0a2", "scaled by a 0-100 percentage");
        put(o, 0x12e, IntegerDataType.dataType, 4, "f12e", "derived from f0a2, minimum 1");
        put(o, 0x132, IntegerDataType.dataType, 4, "flags132", "ActivateObject ors 0x10000; SUB_9 tests it");
        put(o, 0x164, new PointerDataType(VoidDataType.dataType), 4, "f164",
                "copied into slot+4; *(f164+8) into slot+8");
        put(o, 0x190, UnsignedShortDataType.dataType, 2, "frameIndex", "same offset as Effect::frameIndex");
        put(o, 0x194, new PointerDataType(VoidDataType.dataType), 4, "animList", "same offset as Effect::animList");
        put(o, 0x19c, Undefined1DataType.dataType, 1, "loopFlag", null);
        put(o, 0x19d, Undefined1DataType.dataType, 1, "f19d", "set from *(f1a4+0xf)");
        put(o, 0x1a4, new PointerDataType(VoidDataType.dataType), 4, "f1a4", "bytes +0xe/+0xf copied into loopFlag/f19d");
        put(o, 0x1b0, IntegerDataType.dataType, 4, "f1b0", "zeroed by ActivateObject");
        put(o, 0x1b4, IntegerDataType.dataType, 4, "f1b4", "zeroed by ActivateObject");
        put(o, 0x1b8, IntegerDataType.dataType, 4, "f1b8", "zeroed by ActivateObject");
        put(o, 0x1bc, IntegerDataType.dataType, 4, "f1bc", "zeroed by ActivateObject");
        return o;
    }

    private String ctype(DataType dt) {
        String n = dt.getName();
        if (dt instanceof Undefined1DataType) return "unsigned char";
        if (n.equals("undefined") || n.startsWith("undefined")) return null;
        if (dt instanceof PointerDataType) return "void *";
        if (n.equals("int")) return "int";
        if (n.equals("ushort")) return "unsigned short";
        if (n.equals("byte")) return "unsigned char";
        return n;
    }

    private String header(StructureDataType st) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        sb.append("typedef struct ").append(st.getName()).append(" {\n");
        int expect = 0;
        for (DataTypeComponent c : st.getComponents()) {
            DataType dt = c.getDataType();
            String ty = ctype(dt);
            if (ty == null) continue;                       // gap: emitted as one pad run
            int off = c.getOffset();
            if (off > expect) {
                sb.append(String.format("    uint8_t pad_%03X[0x%X];%n", expect, off - expect).replace("%n", "\n"));
            }
            String name = c.getFieldName() == null ? "field" : c.getFieldName();
            if (dt instanceof ArrayDataType) {
                ArrayDataType ad = (ArrayDataType) dt;
                String et = ctype(ad.getDataType());
                sb.append(String.format("    %s %s[%d];", et, name, ad.getNumElements()));
            } else {
                sb.append(String.format("    %s %s;", ty, name));
            }
            if (c.getComment() != null) sb.append("   /* ").append(c.getComment()).append(" */");
            sb.append("\n");
            expect = off + c.getLength();
        }
        if (expect < st.getLength()) {
            sb.append(String.format("    uint8_t pad_%03X[0x%X];\n", expect, st.getLength() - expect));
        }
        sb.append("} ").append(st.getName()).append(";\t/* size 0x")
          .append(Integer.toHexString(st.getLength())).append(" */\n");
        return sb.toString();
    }

    private void applyArray(long base, DataType elem, int count, String name) throws Exception {
        Address a = currentProgram.getAddressFactory().getDefaultAddressSpace().getAddress(base);
        clearListing(a, a.add((long) count * elem.getLength() - 1));
        try {
            createData(a, new ArrayDataType(elem, count, elem.getLength()));
            println("APPLIED " + elem.getName() + "[" + count + "] at " + a);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            println("APPLY FAILED at " + a + ": " + e.getMessage());
        }
        ghidra.program.model.symbol.Symbol sym = getSymbolAt(a);
        if (sym != null) {
            sym.setName(name, ghidra.program.model.symbol.SourceType.USER_DEFINED);
            println("renamed " + a + " -> " + name);
        }
    }

    private void renameData(long addr, String name) throws Exception {
        Address a = currentProgram.getAddressFactory().getDefaultAddressSpace().getAddress(addr);
        ghidra.program.model.symbol.Symbol sym = getSymbolAt(a);
        if (sym == null) { println("no symbol at " + a); return; }
        sym.setName(name, ghidra.program.model.symbol.SourceType.USER_DEFINED);
        println("renamed " + a + " -> " + name);
    }

    @Override
    public void run() throws Exception {
        dtm = currentProgram.getDataTypeManager();

        StructureDataType effect = buildEffect();
        StructureDataType slot = buildSlot(effect);
        StructureDataType obj = buildObj();

        DataType eReg = dtm.addDataType(effect, DataTypeConflictHandler.REPLACE_HANDLER);
        DataType sReg = dtm.addDataType(slot, DataTypeConflictHandler.REPLACE_HANDLER);
        DataType oReg = dtm.addDataType(obj, DataTypeConflictHandler.REPLACE_HANDLER);
        println("STRUCT Effect size=0x" + Integer.toHexString(eReg.getLength()));
        println("STRUCT ObjSlot size=0x" + Integer.toHexString(sReg.getLength()));
        println("STRUCT Obj size=0x" + Integer.toHexString(oReg.getLength()));

        applyArray(0x4bf040L, sReg, 13, "g_objSlots");                        // slot array
        applyArray(0x4de628L, new PointerDataType(oReg), 13, "g_levelObjs");  // Obj* array
        renameData(0x4de620L, "g_levelObjCount");

        new File(OUT).mkdirs();
        File h = new File(OUT + "objslot.h");
        try (PrintWriter pw = new PrintWriter(h, "UTF-8")) {
            pw.println("/* Septerra Core 1.04 object slot -- derived from BuildObjTables (0x428390)");
            pw.println("   and StartSlotEffects (0x4299c0). Offsets verified against the disassembly. */");
            pw.println();
            pw.print(header(effect));
            pw.println();
            pw.print(header(obj));
            pw.println();
            pw.print(header(slot));
        }
        println("HEADER written " + h.getAbsolutePath());
    }
}

```

`port/ghidra/TypeState.java`:

```java
import ghidra.app.script.GhidraScript;
import ghidra.program.model.address.Address;
import ghidra.program.model.data.DataType;
import ghidra.program.model.data.DataTypeConflictHandler;
import ghidra.program.model.data.EnumDataType;
import ghidra.program.model.data.IntegerDataType;
import ghidra.program.model.listing.Data;
import ghidra.program.model.listing.Listing;
import ghidra.program.model.symbol.SourceType;
import ghidra.program.model.symbol.Symbol;
import ghidra.program.model.symbol.SymbolTable;

/**
 * Step 1 of the readability pass: give the state machine real types.
 *
 *   DAT_004ae978 -> g_gameState  (enum GameState)
 *   DAT_004ae960 -> g_shutdown   (int)
 *
 * Enum member names are deliberately neutral (STATE_n): we know the numeric states from the
 * dispatch in WinMain_ and FUN_00430500, but not what they mean yet. Do not invent semantics.
 *
 * Applying the enum is what kills the "(int *)0x0" case labels and the "+ -5" arithmetic,
 * because Ghidra currently infers the global as a pointer.
 *
 * Usage: -postScript TypeState.java
 */
public class TypeState extends GhidraScript {

    private Address addr(long a) {
        return currentProgram.getAddressFactory().getDefaultAddressSpace().getAddress(a);
    }

    private void rename(Address a, String name) {
        SymbolTable st = currentProgram.getSymbolTable();
        Symbol s = st.getPrimarySymbol(a);
        try {
            if (s == null) {
                st.createLabel(a, name, SourceType.USER_DEFINED);
                println("  created label " + name + " @ " + a);
            } else {
                String old = s.getName();
                s.setName(name, SourceType.USER_DEFINED);
                println("  renamed " + old + " -> " + name + " @ " + a);
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            println("  rename FAILED @ " + a + ": " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private void applyType(Address a, DataType dt, int len) {
        try {
            Listing l = currentProgram.getListing();
            if (getInstructionAt(a) != null) { println("  SKIP (code) @ " + a); return; }
            Data d = getDataAt(a);
            if (d != null && d.getDataType().isEquivalent(dt)) { println("  already typed @ " + a); return; }
            if (d != null) clearListing(a, a.add(len - 1));
            createData(a, dt);
            Data n = getDataAt(a);
            println("  typed " + a + " as " + (n == null ? "?" : n.getDataType().getName()));
        } catch (Exception e) {
            println("  applyType FAILED @ " + a + ": " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void run() throws Exception {
        Address stateAddr = addr(0x4ae978);
        Address shutAddr = addr(0x4ae960);

        // --- enum GameState ---------------------------------------------------
        EnumDataType gs = new EnumDataType("GameState", 4);
        // states observed: dispatch in WinMain_ (2, 3-5, 0xb, 0xd, 0xe) and inner cases 0-4
        int[] seen = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 11, 13, 14};
        for (int v : seen) gs.add("STATE_" + v, v);
        DataType registered = currentProgram.getDataTypeManager()
                .addDataType(gs, DataTypeConflictHandler.REPLACE_HANDLER);
        println("enum GameState registered with " + seen.length + " members");

        // --- function names established from the decompiles --------------------
        Object[][] fns = new Object[][] {
            {0x428370, "ClearObjTables"},   // zeroes all four counts
            {0x428390, "BuildObjTables"},   // memset 0xbe8 per slot, slot[0]=object ptr
            {0x428ec0, "ActivateObject"},   // sets active bit, scales a value by a 0-100 pct
            {0x4299c0, "StartSlotEffects"}, // starts animations on slot sub-structs
            {0x40e900, "StartAnimation"},   // frame lookup (stride 0x230) + append to list
        };
        for (Object[] fn : fns) {
            ghidra.program.model.listing.Function f =
                    currentProgram.getFunctionManager().getFunctionAt(addr((Integer) fn[0]));
            if (f == null) { println("  no function at " + fn[0]); continue; }
            f.setName((String) fn[1], ghidra.program.model.symbol.SourceType.USER_DEFINED);
            println("  named function " + f.getEntryPoint() + " -> " + f.getName());
        }

        // --- object tables (established from FUN_00430500's update phases) -----
        // Each count is walked together with one base array; the base of each slot
        // holds a pointer to the object itself. Pairing is documented in the finding.
        Object[][] tabs = new Object[][] {
            {0x4af8cc, "g_objCount0"}, {0x4bf040, "g_objTable0"},
            {0x4af8d0, "g_objCount1"}, {0x4c90a0, "g_objTable1"},
            {0x4af8c4, "g_objCount2"}, {0x4c9100, "g_objTable2"},
            {0x4af8c8, "g_objCount3"}, {0x4c13f8, "g_objTable3"},
        };
        for (Object[] t : tabs) {
            rename(addr((Integer) t[0]), (String) t[1]);
        }

        // --- enum SubState (the second state variable, DAT_004af898) -----------
        // FUN_00430500 switches on (g_subState - 8) and (g_subState - 1): a phase
        // counter within the in-game state. Values observed 1..10; range 0..15 typed.
        EnumDataType ss = new EnumDataType("SubState", 4);
        for (int v = 0; v <= 15; v++) ss.add("SUB_" + v, v);
        DataType ssReg = currentProgram.getDataTypeManager()
                .addDataType(ss, DataTypeConflictHandler.REPLACE_HANDLER);
        println("enum SubState registered with 16 members");

        // --- renames ----------------------------------------------------------
        println("renaming globals:");
        rename(stateAddr, "g_gameState");
        rename(shutAddr, "g_flag_ae960");   // never written by analysed code; name kept neutral
        Address subAddr = addr(0x4af898);
        rename(subAddr, "g_subState");

        // --- types ------------------------------------------------------------
        println("applying types:");
        applyType(stateAddr, registered, 4);
        DataType int4 = ghidra.program.model.data.IntegerDataType.dataType;
        applyType(shutAddr, int4, 4);
        applyType(addr(0x4af898), ssReg, 4);

        println("TYPESTATE done");
    }
}

```

`port/ghidra/WhoCalls.java`:

```java
import ghidra.app.script.GhidraScript;
import ghidra.program.model.address.Address;
import ghidra.program.model.listing.Function;
import ghidra.program.model.listing.Instruction;
import ghidra.program.model.symbol.Reference;
import ghidra.program.model.symbol.Symbol;
import ghidra.program.model.symbol.SymbolIterator;
import ghidra.program.model.symbol.SymbolTable;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.TreeMap;

/**
 * Who calls this API? Handles both direct calls and calls through the import pointer table
 * (the usual shape: instruction -> IAT slot -> external symbol).
 *
 * Usage: -postScript WhoCalls.java BitBlt,StretchBlt,CreateCompatibleBitmap,IDirectDraw_CreateSurface
 */
public class WhoCalls extends GhidraScript {

    private Map<String, List<String>> callers(Address target) {
        Map<String, Integer> counts = new TreeMap<>();
        for (Reference r : currentProgram.getReferenceManager().getReferencesTo(target)) {
            Address from = r.getFromAddress();
            Instruction insn = getInstructionAt(from);
            if (insn != null) {
                Function f = getFunctionContaining(from);
                counts.merge(f == null ? "<no function>" : f.getName(), 1, Integer::sum);
                continue;
            }
            // from a data slot (IAT): find who references the slot
            for (Reference r2 : currentProgram.getReferenceManager().getReferencesTo(from)) {
                Instruction insn2 = getInstructionAt(r2.getFromAddress());
                if (insn2 == null) continue;
                Function f = getFunctionContaining(r2.getFromAddress());
                counts.merge(f == null ? "<no function>" : f.getName(), 1, Integer::sum);
            }
        }
        Map<String, List<String>> out = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        for (Map.Entry<String, Integer> e : counts.entrySet()) {
            List<String> v = new ArrayList<>();
            v.add(String.valueOf(e.getValue()));
            out.put(e.getKey(), v);
        }
        return out;
    }

    @Override
    public void run() throws Exception {
        List<String> names = new ArrayList<>();
        for (String a : getScriptArgs()) for (String p : a.split(",")) if (!p.trim().isEmpty()) names.add(p.trim());
        SymbolTable st = currentProgram.getSymbolTable();

        for (String name : names) {
            Symbol sym = null;
            SymbolIterator it = st.getSymbols(name);
            while (it.hasNext()) {
                Symbol s = it.next();
                if (s.getAddress() != null) { sym = s; break; }
            }
            if (sym == null) { println("MISSING " + name); continue; }
            Map<String, List<String>> c = callers(sym.getAddress());
            if (c.isEmpty()) { println("NOCALLERS " + name + " @" + sym.getAddress()); continue; }
            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder("CALLERS " + name + " (" + c.size() + "):");
            for (Map.Entry<String, List<String>> e : c.entrySet())
                sb.append(" ").append(e.getKey()).append("x").append(e.getValue().get(0));
            println(sb.toString());
        }
    }
}

```

`port/ghidra/Xrefs.java`:

```java
import ghidra.app.script.GhidraScript;
import ghidra.program.model.address.Address;
import ghidra.program.model.listing.Function;
import ghidra.program.model.listing.Instruction;
import ghidra.program.model.symbol.Reference;

import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.TreeMap;

/**
 * Who touches this global? For each address given, list the functions that reference it,
 * with read/write counts and one sample instruction per function. Writes = "MOV [addr], ...",
 * which is how you tell a flag that is set from one that is only tested.
 *
 * Usage: -postScript Xrefs.java 0x4ae960,0x4af8cc,0x4af8d0,0x4af8c4,0x4af8c8,0x4c90a0
 */
public class Xrefs extends GhidraScript {

    @Override
    public void run() throws Exception {
        java.util.List<String> specs = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
        for (String a0 : getScriptArgs()) for (String piece : a0.split(","))
            if (!piece.trim().isEmpty()) specs.add(piece.trim());
        if (specs.isEmpty()) specs.add("0x4ae960");
        for (String spec : specs) {
            Address a = currentProgram.getAddressFactory().getDefaultAddressSpace()
                    .getAddress(Long.parseLong(spec.replace("0x", ""), 16));

            Map<String, int[]> perFn = new TreeMap<>();   // name -> {reads, writes}
            Map<String, String> sample = new LinkedHashMap<>();
            int total = 0;

            for (Reference r : currentProgram.getReferenceManager().getReferencesTo(a)) {
                Instruction insn = getInstructionAt(r.getFromAddress());
                if (insn == null) continue;
                String mnem = insn.getMnemonicString().toUpperCase();
                String txt = insn.toString();
                // CMP/TEST only read; MOV/arithmetic with the address in operand 0 write.
                boolean isWrite = false;
                if (!mnem.equals("CMP") && !mnem.equals("TEST") && !mnem.equals("PUSH")) {
                    String[] ops = txt.split("\\s+", 2);
                    if (ops.length > 1) {
                        String dest = ops[1].split(",")[0].toLowerCase();
                        isWrite = dest.contains("0x" + Long.toHexString(a.getOffset()));
                    }
                }
                Function f = getFunctionContaining(r.getFromAddress());
                String fn = (f == null) ? "<no function>" : f.getName();
                int[] c = perFn.computeIfAbsent(fn, k -> new int[2]);
                if (isWrite) c[1]++; else c[0]++;
                sample.putIfAbsent(fn, mnem + " " + txt);
                total++;
            }

            println("=== XREFS " + a + " (" + spec + ") refs=" + total + " functions=" + perFn.size());
            for (Map.Entry<String, int[]> e : perFn.entrySet()) {
                println("   " + e.getKey() + "  read=" + e.getValue()[0] + " write=" + e.getValue()[1] +
                        "   e.g. " + sample.get(e.getKey()));
            }
        }
    }
}

`port/ghidra/finding_430500_state_machine.md`:

```md
# Finding: two-level state machine over fixed object slots

Decompiled `FUN_00430500` and its caller `WinMain_` (`0x412b80`) to answer one question:
can this engine be cut into chunks? Then named what the code proved.

Answer: **yes — a two-level state machine driving 0xBE8-byte object slots.**

## Level 1 — `WinMain_` (546 lines of C)

```c
if (g_flag_ae960 == 0) {
    ...
    if (g_gameState == STATE_2)          { FUN_004156a0(); }
    else if (g_gameState - STATE_3 < 3)  { FUN_00430500(iVar4, iVar8); }   /* states 3,4,5 */
    FUN_004046e0();
}
```

`STATE_11`, `STATE_13`, `STATE_14` and `(int)g_gameState < 6` gate other calls, so 0–5 are one
family and 11/13/14 another.

## Level 2 — the phase machine in `FUN_00430500` (1,090 lines)

| phase | what it does |
|---|---|
| `SUB_8` (case 0) | **spawn / level-entry pass** — `ActivateObject(0)` per entry of `g_objCount0`, then `StartSlotEffects()` per entry of `g_objCount1`; ends by setting `g_subState = SUB_9` |
| `SUB_9` (case 1) | **per-frame update** — scans table 0 for `+0x16c == 0`; in table 2, if slot flag `+0x18 & 1` clear and `*slot == 1` → `FUN_00434be0`, and if `+0x5c == 4` → `FUN_0042c200` |
| `SUB_10` (case 2) | message/wait handling; ends by calling `FUN_0042a780`, which tail-jumps into `BuildObjTables` |
| `SUB_12` (case 4) | frame-timing gate — `DAT_004af834/830` vs `DAT_004aea14/a10`, plus ESC (`DAT_004aef88 == 0x1b`) → `FUN_00410600(1)` |

Correction to the earlier reading: `SUB_8` is **not** the update tick. It runs once on level
entry, activates every object, kicks off each slot's animations, then advances itself to
`SUB_9`. `SUB_9` is the per-frame path.

## The object slot: 0xBE8 bytes

`BuildObjTables` (0x428390) proves the layout arithmetically:

```c
ClearObjTables();
g_objCount1 = g_objCount0 = DAT_004de620;        /* count from the loaded level */
puVar3 = &DAT_004bf048;                          /* table 0 + 8 */
do {
    _Dst = puVar3 - 2;
    memset(_Dst, 0, 0xbe8);                      /* slot size = 0xBE8          */
    iVar1 = (&DAT_004de628)[uVar4];              /* source object pointer list */
    *_Dst = iVar1;                               /* slot[0] = object           */
    puVar3[-1] = *(int *)(iVar1 + 0x164);        /* slot[1] = obj->0x164       */
    *puVar3    = *(int *)(*(int*)(iVar1+0x164) + 8);   /* slot[2] */
    (&g_objTable1)[uVar4] = _Dst;                /* table 1 = slot pointers    */
    puVar3[0x2c2] = 0xffffffff;                  /* slot+0xB10 = -1 (no effect)*/
    puVar3[0x2f1] = 1; puVar3[0x2f2] = 1;        /* slot+0xBCC, +0xBD0         */
    uVar4++; puVar3 += 0x2fa;                    /* stride 0x2fa ints = 0xBE8  */
} while (uVar4 < uVar2);
```

Two independent confirmations of 0xBE8: the `memset` length and the `+0x2fa` int stride.

### Slot layout

| offset | contents |
|---|---|
| `0x000` | object pointer |
| `0x004` | `obj->0x164` |
| `0x008` | `*(obj->0x164 + 8)` |
| `0x228` | **5 sub-structs of 0x1C8 bytes each** (`0x228 + 5*0x1C8 = 0xB10`) |
| `0xB10` | active-effect selector, `-1` = none |
| `0xBCC`, `0xBD0` | initialised to 1 |

The 5×0x1C8 block is proven by `StartSlotEffects`, which calls
`StartAnimation(slot + 0x228 / 0x3f0 / 0x5b8 / 0x780 / 0x948, 500, 0, 0)` — differences of
exactly `0x1C8` — and switches on the selector at `slot + 0xB10`.

### Struct types — defined in the database, emitted as C

`port/include/objslot.h` (generated by `Structs.java`), applied in Ghidra as `ObjSlot[13]`
at `0x4bf040`. `BuildObjTables` now decompiles as typed code:

```c
ObjSlot *_Dst;
piVar4 = &g_objSlots[0].f008;
_Dst = (ObjSlot *)(piVar4 + -2);
_Dst->obj = pvVar1;
```

```c
typedef struct Effect {            /* 0x1C8 */
    uint8_t pad_000[0x40];
    void *  animRoot;              /* resource/animation root            */
    unsigned short f044;
    uint8_t pad_046[0x16];
    int     state;                 /* StartSlotEffects writes 2          */
    int     f060;                  /* StartSlotEffects writes 2          */
    uint8_t pad_064[0x8];
    int     f06c;                  /* StartSlotEffects writes 1          */
    uint8_t pad_070[0x120];
    unsigned short frameIndex;     /* index into 0x230-byte frame table  */
    uint8_t pad_192[0x2];
    void *  animList;              /* StartAnimation appends here        */
    uint8_t pad_198[0xC];
    void *  f1a4;                  /* bytes +0xe/+0xf copied into obj    */
    uint8_t pad_1A8[0x20];
} Effect;

typedef struct ObjSlot {           /* 0xBE8 */
    void *  obj;                   /* object pointer                     */
    int     animRoot;              /* copy of obj->0x164                 */
    int     f008;                  /* *(obj->0x164 + 8)                  */
    uint8_t pad_00C[0x21C];
    Effect  effects[5];
    int     activeEffect;          /* -1 = none; indexes effects[]       */
    uint8_t pad_B14[0xB8];
    int     fBCC;                  /* initialised to 1                   */
    int     fBD0;                  /* initialised to 1                   */
    uint8_t pad_BD4[0x14];
} ObjSlot;
```

### Obj — the object's first 0x1C8 bytes are the Effect layout

Every offset `ActivateObject` and `StartAnimation` touch on an object is below `0x1C8`, which
is exactly `sizeof(Effect)`: `+0x18` (byte flags), `+0x40` animRoot, `+0x44`, `+0x5c` state,
`+0x60`, `+0x6c`, `+0xa2` (short, scaled by a percentage), `+0x12e`, `+0x132` flags,
`+0x164` -> slot+4, `+0x190` frameIndex, `+0x194` animList, `+0x19c`, `+0x19d`, `+0x1a4`,
`+0x1b0..0x1bc` (four dwords zeroed). So `Obj` (0x1C8) shares the animatable header with
`Effect` — same names at the same offsets. `port/include/objslot.h` carries all three structs.

Applied: `Obj *[13]` at `0x4de628` (`g_levelObjs`), count at `0x4de620`
(`g_levelObjCount`). `BuildObjTables` now reads `pOVar1 = g_levelObjs[uVar5]; _Dst->obj = pOVar1;`.

Retyping the decompiler's locals in `ActivateObject` (`in_EAX` -> `ObjSlot *`, `iVar1` -> `Obj *`)
turns it from offset soup into field names:

```c
Obj *pObj;  ObjSlot *in_EAX;
pObj = in_EAX->obj;
if (((pObj->flags132 & 0x10000U) == 0) || ((pObj->flags0 & 8) == 0)) {
    pObj->flags132 = pObj->flags132 | 0x10000;
    ...
    pObj->loopFlag = *(undefined1 *)((int)pvVar1 + 0xe);
    pObj->f19d     = *(undefined1 *)((int)pvVar1 + 0xf);
```

Two notes for anyone repeating this: relabelling a *global's* type never fixes variable-typed
noise (only the variable's type does), and **Ghidra splits script arguments on `=`** — pass
`var:Type`, not `var=Type`, or every assignment silently becomes two bad arguments.

New detail from the retyped output: `ActivateObject` also zeroes `slot+0xB34`, a field of the
slot beyond `activeEffect` that the struct does not name yet.

### Correction: `0x4c13f8` is not a fourth table — it is `&g_objSlots[3]`

`0x4c13f8 - 0x4bf040 = 0x23B8 = 3 * 0xBE8` exactly, and the loop starting there strides
`piVar12 + 0x2fa` ints — the same slot stride. So the earlier `g_objTable0` / `g_objTable3`
split was wrong: there is **one slot array** at 0x4bf040, and the `g_objCount3` loop walks it
from index 3. Hypothesis (unverified): slots 0-2 are the party, 3+ everything else.

Likewise `0x4c90a0` is a **pointer array of slot pointers** — disassembly shows
`mov %ebx,0x4c90a0(,%edi,4)` — now named `g_slotPtrs`. `0x4c9100` begins 24 dwords later and
is a separate array of 0xB34-byte entries (still `g_objTable2`, semantics unknown).

### Slot array size: 13 slots

The slot array must end before `g_slotPtrs`: `0x4c90a0 - 0x4bf040 = 41,056` bytes = 13 slots
(39,624) plus 1,432 spare. That bounds a scene at **13 concurrent object slots** — worth
checking against the level loader (`DAT_004de620`) before relying on it.

### Function names established

| address | name | evidence |
|---|---|---|
| 0x428370 | `ClearObjTables` | zeroes all four counts |
| 0x428390 | `BuildObjTables` | the slot-filling loop above |
| 0x428ec0 | `ActivateObject(pct)` | sets `+0x132 |= 0x10000`, `+0x18` bits, scales `*(short*)(obj+0xa2)` by a 0–100 percentage into `+0x12e` |
| 0x4299c0 | `StartSlotEffects` | the 5×0x1C8 animation starts |
| 0x40e900 | `StartAnimation(ptr, ms, …)` | frame lookup `*(ushort*)(ptr+0x190) * 0x230 + *(*ptr+0x40 + 0x18)`, appends to a list at `+0x194` |

`BuildObjTables` is reached by **tail jumps** from `FUN_0042a780` and `FUN_00406f30`, so it is
a shared level-load tail, not a normal callee.

### Object field map (offsets seen so far)

`+0x18` byte flags (`& 8`, `& 0xffffeffe`), `+0x5c` state (== 4), `+0xa2` short scaled by
percentage, `+0x12e` derived divisor, `+0x132` flags (`| 0x10000`), `+0x164` → animation/resource
root, `+0x16c` counter gating table 0, `+0x190` frame index, `+0x194` animation list head,
`+0x19c` loop flag, `+0x1a4` → bytes at `+0xe`/`+0xf`. Objects and the 0x1C8 effect structs
share this header shape — same "animatable" base.

## Flags — corrected and still open

`g_shutdown` → **`g_flag_ae960`**: 19 references across 6 functions, **all compares, zero
writes**, initial value 0 in the file image. Never set by analysed code: the `do/while` runs
once and the `if (…) break` never fires on it. Neutral name kept. Caveat: undisassembled
`.text` or a computed store could hide a writer, so this is "no writes found".

All four counts/tables live in `.bss`; only `g_gameState` (0x4ae978) and `g_flag_ae960`
(0x4ae960) are initialised data, both 0.

## Typing applied — `port/ghidra/TypeState.java` (idempotent)

`g_gameState` (`enum GameState`), `g_subState` (`enum SubState`), `g_flag_ae960`,
`g_objCount0…3`, `g_objTable0…3`, plus the five function names above.

## Negative result: retyping the switch variable does not clear the case labels

`piVar9` (`int *`) → `int`: 1,089 → 1,091 lines, still 8/8 `case (int *)0xN`, variable renamed
throughout. Reverted. There is no named switch variable — Ghidra emits
`switch((int *)(g_subState - SUB_8))` inline, so the cast is its own jump-table inference.

## Combat lists — named by the game's own assert strings

`AddCombatant` (0x428240, `FUN_00428240`) is the only writer of `g_enemyCount` and
`g_allyCount` besides `ClearObjTables`, and it names itself:

```c
if (param_2 != 0) {                              /* ally */
    if (9 < g_objCount3)
        FUN_004129d0("Tell Jim you ran out of slots in the Allies Combat List",0x70);
    memset(&g_objSlots[g_allyCount + 3],0,0xbe8);
    g_objSlots[g_allyCount + 3].obj = obj;
    ...
} else {                                         /* enemy */
    if (9 < g_objCount2)
        FUN_004129d0("Tell Jim you ran out of slots in the Enemies Combat List",0x70);
    memset(&g_enemySlots[g_enemyCount],0,0xbe8);
    g_enemySlots[g_enemyCount].obj = obj;
}
```

So, with the embedded strings as the authority:

- `g_allyCount` (0x4af8c8) counts the **Allies Combat List**, capped at 10, stored in
  `g_objSlots[3..12]` — which **confirms** slots 0–2 are reserved (the party).
- `g_enemyCount` (0x4af8c4) counts the **Enemies Combat List**, capped at 10, stored in a
  **second slot array** `g_enemySlots` (0x4c9100) with the same 0xBE8 stride — the earlier
  "0xB34-byte entries" reading was wrong; 0xB34 is a field inside a slot.
- Registration gates: `obj != 0`, `*(int*)(obj+0x164) != 0`, `FUN_004239b0(obj) != 0`,
  `obj->flags132 & 0x10000` set, `obj->flags0 & 1` clear.
- Slot fields written here: `+0xB2C` (the `param_3` argument), `+0xBCC`, `+0xBD0`.

That makes 13 + 10 = 23 slot objects alive in the worst case, and explains why 13 slots fit
before `g_slotPtrs`.

## Next steps

1. **Define the struct in Ghidra** — `ObjSlot` (0xBE8) with a 5×0x1C8 effect array and the
   0xB10 selector, then apply it to the tables. This is now cheap and will clean up every
   loop that walks a slot.
2. `FUN_0042c200` and `FUN_00434be0` — the two conditional calls in `SUB_9`.
3. `FUN_004156a0` (3.4 KB, `STATE_2`) — the other half of level 1.

`port/ghidra/make_symbol_map.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Build the symbol -> subsystem map that CallTree.java uses.

The SR-Septerra shim headers are the authoritative list of what the game calls across
the asm/C boundary, and the header name tells you which subsystem each symbol belongs to.
Names are emitted raw and with the SRW suffixes stripped (_asm2c / _c2asm / _c), because
Ghidra shows them either way.

Writes <out>/shim_symbols.csv  (symbol,base,subsystem)
Run from the SR-Septerra source dir or pass it as argv[1].
"""
import collections
import os
import re
import sys

HERE = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "H:/ghidra/reference/SR/games/Septerra Core/SR-Septerra"
OUT = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "H:/ghidra/work/ghidra-out/shim_symbols.csv"

SUBSYSTEM = {
    "WinApi-ddraw.h": "graphics", "WinApi-dsound.h": "audio", "WinApi-gdi32.h": "gdi",
    "WinApi-kernel32.h": "os", "WinApi-user32.h": "ui", "WinApi-winmm.h": "timer",
    "QTML.h": "video", "MSS.h": "audio", "Game-DataFiles.h": "assets",
    "Game-Registry.h": "config", "Game-Config.h": "config", "Game-SoundEngine.h": "audio",
    "CLIB.h": "crt", "Game-Memory.h": "memory", "Game-Patch.h": "patch", "platform.h": "crt",
}

DECL = re.compile(r"\b(CCALL|STDCALL)\s+([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)\s*\(")


def base(n):
    for suf in ("_asm2c", "_c2asm", "_c"):
        if n.endswith(suf):
            return n[: -len(suf)]
    return n


def main():
    found = {}
    for header, sub in SUBSYSTEM.items():
        path = os.path.join(HERE, header)
        if not os.path.exists(path):
            continue
        for line in open(path, encoding="latin1"):
            if line.lstrip().startswith(("//", "*")):
                continue
            for m in DECL.finditer(line):
                found.setdefault(m.group(2), sub)

    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(OUT), exist_ok=True)
    with open(OUT, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as f:
        f.write("symbol,base,subsystem\n")
        for name, sub in sorted(found.items()):
            f.write(f"{name},{base(name)},{sub}\n")

    print(f"wrote {OUT}: {len(found)} symbols")
    for sub, n in collections.Counter(found.values()).most_common():
        print(f"  {sub:<10} {n}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`port/ghidra/subsystem_map.md`:

```md
# Subsystem map — original Septerra Core 1.04

Produced from the Ghidra program `work/ghidra-proj/Septerra` (1,335 functions, 429,588 bytes)
with `port/ghidra/CallTree.java`, walking the call graph down from `WinMain_` @ `0x412b80`.
Raw data: `work/ghidra-out/calltree_nodes.csv`, `calltree_edges.csv`, `annotation.csv`.

## Headline numbers

| | functions | bytes |
|---|---|---|
| whole program | 1,335 | 429,588 |
| reachable from `WinMain_`, depth ≤ 6 | 743 | 264,380 |
| — of those, calling **no** boundary shim (pure engine logic) | **578** | **163,531** |
| not reached by the walk | 592 | — |

Depth profile: `0=1, 1=83, 2=213, 3=176, 4=167, 5=79, 6=24`.

## Where the boundary calls go

Functions (among the 743) whose *direct* callees touch each subsystem:

| subsystem | functions | bytes |
|---|---|---|
| crt | 104 | 63,564 |
| ui (`user32`) | 29 | 50,002 |
| os (`kernel32`: files, registry, threads) | 30 | 13,627 |
| assets (`Game-DataFiles`) | 14 | 11,619 |
| gdi | 10 | 7,042 |
| audio (`dsound`, `MSS`, SoundEngine) | 6 | 2,264 |
| video (`QTML`) | 6 | 1,357 |
| graphics (`ddraw`) | 1 | 768 |
| config, timer | 2, 2 | 470, 213 |

## Reading this correctly — two caveats

1. **`graphics=1` is not the truth.** DirectDraw must be the game's main output path, so the
   call graph is missing most of it. `getCalledFunctions` only sees *direct* `call` targets;
   the engine dispatches through function pointers and virtual calls (`call [eax+8]`), so
   entire subsystems are invisible to the walk.
2. **That also explains the 592 unreachable functions.** They are not all dead code or CRT —
   a chunk will be indirect-call targets reachable only through pointer tables. Any work plan
   built on this map must treat "unreachable" as "unknown route", not "not needed".

## The 83 direct callees of `WinMain_` (top level)

The largest ones, i.e. the entry points into the real engine:

| address | size | callees | boundary | note |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `00430500` | 5,822 B | 38 | — | biggest pure-engine function, called straight from `WinMain_` |
| `004156a0` | 3,486 B | 14 | ui | UI/dispatch heavy |
| `0040fdf0` | 1,941 B | 30 | assets, crt | asset/manifest init path |
| `00418ce0` | 1,731 B | 35 | gdi, ui | text/UI drawing |
| `00443f50` | 1,727 B | 12 | — | uses `sprintf_asm2c` (`%dx`) |
| `004073f0` | 1,549 B | 17 | — | |
| `00445460` | 1,042 B | 0 | — | `OpenGameDataFiles_asm2c` — already C |
| `00401bc0` | 935 B | 15 | crt, os | startup/IO |
| `00464bb0` | 917 B | 12 | audio, crt, os | sound init |

## Biggest pure-engine functions (the actual decompilation targets)

| address | bytes | callees | note |
|---|---|---|---|
| `00430500` | 5,822 | 38 | depth 1 — strongest first target |
| `0043b180` | 4,660 | 25 | |
| `00437e40` | 4,567 | 25 | |
| `0042b0f0` | 4,099 | 6 | |
| `0042d950` | 4,093 | 11 | uses `_CIpow` |
| `00435f90` | 3,677 | 15 | |
| `0043da10` | 3,556 | 13 | references `"%02d"` (number formatting) |
| `00424350` | 2,947 | 2 | uses `_CIsqrt` |

## What did *not* work, so nobody repeats it

`Annotate.java` tried to name these functions from referenced string literals. Yield was
almost nil — `0043da10 → "%02d"`, `00443f50 → "%dx"`, everything else empty. These are
game-logic and math routines that build data rather than format text, so **string xrefs are
not a viable naming strategy here.** Naming will need data-flow analysis (what globals they
touch, what structs they index) or behaviour diffing against the running recomp.

## Suggested work order

1. `0x430500` first — 5.8 KB, 38 callees, directly under `WinMain_`, no boundary calls: most
   likely the main loop or the game-state machine, and the richest structural anchor.
2. Follow its callees before widening: 38 edges is enough to define a subsystem boundary.
3. Only then look at `0x43b180` / `0x437e40` (4.6 KB each).
4. Resolve the indirect-call blind spot early — export pointer tables from `.data`
   (`relocations.csv` already marks 21,588 pointer locations) so the map stops lying.

```

`port/include/objslot.h`:

```h
/* Septerra Core 1.04 object slot -- derived from BuildObjTables (0x428390)
   and StartSlotEffects (0x4299c0). Offsets verified against the disassembly. */

typedef struct Effect {
    uint8_t pad_000[0x40];
    void * animRoot;   /* resource/animation root */
    unsigned short f044;
    uint8_t pad_046[0x16];
    int state;   /* StartSlotEffects writes 2 */
    int f060;   /* StartSlotEffects writes 2 */
    uint8_t pad_064[0x8];
    int f06c;   /* StartSlotEffects writes 1 */
    uint8_t pad_070[0x120];
    unsigned short frameIndex;   /* index into 0x230-byte frame table */
    uint8_t pad_192[0x2];
    void * animList;   /* StartAnimation appends here */
    uint8_t pad_198[0x4];
    unsigned char loopFlag;   /* StartAnimation picks flag 1 when 0 */
    uint8_t pad_19D[0x7];
    void * f1a4;   /* bytes +0xe/+0xf copied into obj */
    uint8_t pad_1A8[0x20];
} Effect;	/* size 0x1c8 */

typedef struct Obj {
    uint8_t pad_000[0x18];
    unsigned char flags0;   /* byte flags: ActivateObject ors 8 and clears 0x1001; SUB_9 tests bit 1 */
    uint8_t pad_019[0x27];
    void * animRoot;   /* same offset as Effect::animRoot */
    unsigned short f044;
    uint8_t pad_046[0x16];
    int state;   /* == 4 branches in SUB_9 */
    uint8_t pad_060[0xC];
    int f06c;
    uint8_t pad_070[0x32];
    short f0a2;   /* scaled by a 0-100 percentage */
    uint8_t pad_0A4[0x8A];
    int f12e;   /* derived from f0a2, minimum 1 */
    int flags132;   /* ActivateObject ors 0x10000; SUB_9 tests it */
    uint8_t pad_136[0x2E];
    void * f164;   /* copied into slot+4; *(f164+8) into slot+8 */
    uint8_t pad_168[0x28];
    unsigned short frameIndex;   /* same offset as Effect::frameIndex */
    uint8_t pad_192[0x2];
    void * animList;   /* same offset as Effect::animList */
    uint8_t pad_198[0x4];
    unsigned char loopFlag;
    unsigned char f19d;   /* set from *(f1a4+0xf) */
    uint8_t pad_19E[0x6];
    void * f1a4;   /* bytes +0xe/+0xf copied into loopFlag/f19d */
    uint8_t pad_1A8[0x8];
    int f1b0;   /* zeroed by ActivateObject */
    int f1b4;   /* zeroed by ActivateObject */
    int f1b8;   /* zeroed by ActivateObject */
    int f1bc;   /* zeroed by ActivateObject */
    uint8_t pad_1C0[0x8];
} Obj;	/* size 0x1c8 */

typedef struct ObjSlot {
    void * obj;   /* object pointer */
    int animRoot;   /* copy of obj->0x164 */
    int f008;   /* *(obj->0x164 + 8) */
    uint8_t pad_00C[0x21C];
    Effect effects[5];   /* five 0x1C8 effect structs; 0x228 + 5*0x1C8 = 0xB10 */
    int activeEffect;   /* -1 = none; indexes effects[] */
    uint8_t pad_B14[0xB8];
    int fBCC;   /* initialised to 1 */
    int fBD0;   /* initialised to 1 */
    uint8_t pad_BD4[0x14];
} ObjSlot;	/* size 0xbe8 */

`port/patches/0002-legacy-pack-blob-override.patch`:

```patch
diff --git a/games/Septerra Core/SR-Septerra/Game-DataFiles.c b/games/Septerra Core/SR-Septerra/Game-DataFiles.c
index 1221492..70e46d1 100644
--- a/games/Septerra Core/SR-Septerra/Game-DataFiles.c	
+++ b/games/Septerra Core/SR-Septerra/Game-DataFiles.c	
@@ -76,8 +76,148 @@ typedef struct
     uint32_t Offset;        // current offset in record
     int32_t NewOffset;      // new offset
     int32_t NewWhence;      // whence of new offset
+    FILE *PackFile;         // legacy pack override (blob level), NULL = normal
+    uint32_t PackSize;      // size of the override blob
 } RecordInfo;
 
+/* --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+ * Legacy pack override (blob level).
+ *
+ * Enabled by setting LEGACY_PACK_DIR. A record whose key has a file at
+ *   <dir>/blobs/<key>.bin      (8 or 1..8 lowercase hex digits, no 0x)
+ * is read from that file instead of the database, with the pack file's own
+ * size used for RecordGetSize. Anything absent falls through to the archive.
+ *
+ * This is the v0 override point from port/docs/pack_format.md: whole records
+ * only, no decompiled code required. Set LEGACY_PACK_TRACE=1 to log every key
+ * the game opens, which is how you discover keys to override.
+ * -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+static char LegacyPackDir[256];
+static int  LegacyPackState = 0;   /* 0 = not initialised, 1 = enabled, -1 = off */
+
+static void LegacyPackInit(void)
+{
+    const char *env = getenv("LEGACY_PACK_DIR");
+    LegacyPackState = -1;
+    if (env == NULL || env[0] == '\0') return;
+
+    /* tolerate quoting and a trailing separator: set LEGACY_PACK_DIR="D:\packs\" */
+    {
+        const char *src = env;
+        size_t n;
+
+        while (*src == '"' || *src == '\'') src++;
+        snprintf(LegacyPackDir, sizeof(LegacyPackDir), "%s", src);
+        n = strlen(LegacyPackDir);
+        while (n > 0 && (LegacyPackDir[n - 1] == '"' || LegacyPackDir[n - 1] == '\'' ||
+                         LegacyPackDir[n - 1] == '\\' || LegacyPackDir[n - 1] == '/'))
+            LegacyPackDir[--n] = '\0';
+    }
+    LegacyPackState = 1;
+    eprintf("legacy-pack: enabled, dir=%s\n", LegacyPackDir);
+}
+
+static int LegacyPackTrace(void)
+{
+    const char *env = getenv("LEGACY_PACK_TRACE");
+    return (env != NULL && env[0] != '\0' && env[0] != '0');
+}
+
+static const char *LegacyPackDumpDir(void)
+{
+    static char DumpDir[256];
+
+    if (DumpDir[0] != '\0') return DumpDir;
+
+    {
+        const char *env = getenv("LEGACY_PACK_DUMP");
+        const char *src;
+        size_t n;
+
+        if (env == NULL || env[0] == '\0') return NULL;
+
+        src = env;
+        while (*src == '"' || *src == '\'') src++;
+        snprintf(DumpDir, sizeof(DumpDir), "%s", src);
+        n = strlen(DumpDir);
+        while (n > 0 && (DumpDir[n - 1] == '"' || DumpDir[n - 1] == '\'' ||
+                         DumpDir[n - 1] == '\\' || DumpDir[n - 1] == '/'))
+            DumpDir[--n] = '\0';
+        if (DumpDir[0] == '\0') return NULL;
+        eprintf("legacy-pack: dump enabled, dir=%s\n", DumpDir);
+    }
+    return DumpDir;
+}
+
+/* Read a record once, write it out as <dir>/<key>.bin, then rewind it for the caller.
+ * Only runs when LEGACY_PACK_DUMP is set; this is how pack authors obtain the
+ * originals to build a pack from. */
+static void LegacyPackDumpRecord(int32_t RecordHandle, uint32_t RecordKey, uint32_t Size)
+{
+    const char *Dir = LegacyPackDumpDir();
+    char path[512];
+    uint8_t *Buffer;
+    FILE *Out;
+    uint32_t Read;
+
+    if (Dir == NULL || Size == 0) return;
+
+    Buffer = malloc(Size);
+    if (Buffer == NULL) return;
+
+    Read = RecordRead(RecordHandle, Buffer, Size);
+    RecordSeek(RecordHandle, 0, 0);
+
+    snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "%s/%08x.bin", Dir, RecordKey);
+    Out = fopen(path, "wb");
+    if (Out != NULL)
+    {
+        if (Read > 0) fwrite(Buffer, 1, Read, Out);
+        fclose(Out);
+        eprintf("legacy-pack: dumped key 0x%x (%u bytes) -> %s\n", RecordKey, Read, path);
+    }
+    else
+    {
+        eprintf("legacy-pack: dump write failed %s (errno %d)\n", path, errno);
+    }
+
+    free(Buffer);
+}
+
+static FILE *LegacyPackOpenBlob(uint32_t RecordKey, uint32_t *Size)
+{
+    char path[512];
+    FILE *File;
+    long Length;
+
+    if (LegacyPackState == 0) LegacyPackInit();
+    if (LegacyPackState != 1) return NULL;
+
+    snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "%s/blobs/%08x.bin", LegacyPackDir, RecordKey);
+    File = fopen(path, "rb");
+    if (File == NULL)
+    {
+        int FirstError = errno;
+        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "%s/blobs/%x.bin", LegacyPackDir, RecordKey);
+        File = fopen(path, "rb");
+        if (File == NULL)
+        {
+            if (LegacyPackTrace())
+                eprintf("legacy-pack: miss %s (errno %d)\n", path, FirstError);
+            return NULL;
+        }
+    }
+
+    if (fseek(File, 0, SEEK_END) != 0) { eprintf("legacy-pack: seek failed %s (errno %d)\n", path, errno); fclose(File); return NULL; }
+    Length = ftell(File);
+    if (Length < 0) { eprintf("legacy-pack: tell failed %s\n", path); fclose(File); return NULL; }
+    fseek(File, 0, SEEK_SET);
+
+    *Size = (uint32_t)Length;
+    eprintf("legacy-pack: override key 0x%x (%u bytes) <- %s\n", RecordKey, *Size, path);
+    return File;
+}
+
 typedef struct
 {
     uint32_t RecordKey;          // record ID
@@ -448,6 +588,18 @@ int32_t CCALL RecordOpen(uint32_t RecordKey)

+        RecordBuffer[result].PackFile = PackFile;
+        RecordBuffer[result].PackSize = PackSize;
+        if (PackFile == NULL && LegacyPackTrace())
+            eprintf("legacy-pack: trace key 0x%x (size %u, no override)\n", RecordKey,
+                    IndexFileBuffer[IndexNumber].UncompressedSize);
+        if (PackFile == NULL)
+            LegacyPackDumpRecord(result, RecordKey, IndexFileBuffer[IndexNumber].UncompressedSize);

`port/patches/0003-legacy-blit-log.patch`:

```patch
diff --git a/games/Septerra Core/SR-Septerra/WinApi-ddraw.c b/games/Septerra Core/SR-Septerra/WinApi-ddraw.c
index d053947..93a85b0 100644
--- a/games/Septerra Core/SR-Septerra/WinApi-ddraw.c	
+++ b/games/Septerra Core/SR-Septerra/WinApi-ddraw.c	
@@ -1070,8 +1070,100 @@ uint32_t CCALL IDirectDrawSurface_AddOverlayDirtyRect_c(struct IDirectDrawSurfac
 //    return IDirectDrawSurface_AddOverlayDirtyRect(lpThis->lpObject, (LPRECT)param1);
 }
 
+/* ---- legacy blit instrumentation -----------------------------------------
+ * Answers one question: does the wrapper see the engine drawing each sprite?
+ * Set LEGACY_BLIT_LOG=<file path> and play. Every present writes one line:
+ *     frame <n> blits=<n> locks=<n>
+ * plus up to 300 individual blit lines carrying the rectangles.
+ * Many blits with small rectangles -> the wrapper sees per-sprite drawing.
+ * Few blits but many locks         -> the engine writes pixels itself.
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+static FILE *LegacyBlitLog = NULL;
+static int LegacyBlitLogState = 0;
+static unsigned long LegacyBlitCount = 0;
+static unsigned long LegacyLockCount = 0;
+static unsigned long LegacyFrameNo = 0;
+
+static FILE *LegacyBlitLogFile(void)
+{
+    if (LegacyBlitLogState == 0)
+    {
+        const char *Path = getenv("LEGACY_BLIT_LOG");
+
+        LegacyBlitLogState = -1;
+
+        if ((Path != NULL) && (Path[0] != '\0'))
+        {
+            LegacyBlitLog = fopen(Path, "w");
+
+            if (LegacyBlitLog != NULL)
+            {
+                LegacyBlitLogState = 1;
+                fprintf(LegacyBlitLog, "# frame blits locks\n");
+                fflush(LegacyBlitLog);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    return LegacyBlitLog;
+}
+
+static int LegacySurfW(struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *S)
+{
+    return ((S != NULL) && (S->Surface != NULL)) ? S->Surface->w : -1;
+}
+
+static int LegacySurfH(struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *S)
+{
+    return ((S != NULL) && (S->Surface != NULL)) ? S->Surface->h : -1;
+}
+
+static void LegacyBlitMark(struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *Dst, struct _rect *DstRect,
+                           struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *Src, struct _rect *SrcRect)
+{
+    FILE *Log = LegacyBlitLogFile();
+
+    LegacyBlitCount++;
+
+    if ((Log == NULL) || (LegacyBlitCount > 300))
+    {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    fprintf(Log, "blit dst=%dx%d dstrect=(%d,%d,%d,%d) src=%dx%d srcrect=(%d,%d,%d,%d)\n",
+            LegacySurfW(Dst), LegacySurfH(Dst),
+            DstRect ? DstRect->left : -1, DstRect ? DstRect->top : -1,
+            DstRect ? DstRect->right : -1, DstRect ? DstRect->bottom : -1,
+            LegacySurfW(Src), LegacySurfH(Src),
+            SrcRect ? SrcRect->left : -1, SrcRect ? SrcRect->top : -1,
+            SrcRect ? SrcRect->right : -1, SrcRect ? SrcRect->bottom : -1);
+}
+
+static void LegacyLockMark(void)
+{
+    LegacyLockCount++;
+}
+
+static void LegacyFrameEnd(void)
+{
+    FILE *Log = LegacyBlitLogFile();
+
+    if (Log == NULL)
+    {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    LegacyFrameNo++;
+    fprintf(Log, "frame %lu blits=%lu locks=%lu\n", LegacyFrameNo, LegacyBlitCount, LegacyLockCount);
+    fflush(Log);
+
+    LegacyBlitCount = 0;
+    LegacyLockCount = 0;
+}
+
 uint32_t CCALL IDirectDrawSurface_Blt_c(struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *lpThis, struct _rect *lpDestRect, struct IDirectDrawSurface_c * lpDDSrcSurface, struct _rect *lpSrcRect, uint32_t dwFlags, struct _ddbltfx * lpDDBltFX)
 {
+    LegacyBlitMark(lpThis, lpDestRect, lpDDSrcSurface, lpSrcRect);
     SDL_Rect dstrect, srcrect;
     int fill_first;

 {
+    LegacyFrameEnd();
 #ifdef DEBUG_DDRAW
     eprintf("IDirectDrawSurface_Flip: 0x%" PRIxPTR ", 0x%" PRIxPTR ", 0x%x - ", (uintptr_t)lpThis, (uintptr_t)lpDDSurfaceTargetOverride, dwFlags);
 #endif
@@ -1690,6 +1783,7 @@ uint32_t CCALL IDirectDrawSurface_IsLost_c(struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *lpThis)

+static unsigned long LegacyFrameNo = 0;

+static FILE *LegacyBlitLogFile(void)
+{

+            {
+                LegacyBlitLogState = 1;
+                fprintf(LegacyBlitLog, "# frame blits locks\n");
+                fflush(LegacyBlitLog);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    return LegacyBlitLog;
+}
+
+static int LegacySurfW(struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *S)
+{
+    return ((S != NULL) && (S->Surface != NULL)) ? S->Surface->w : -1;
+}
+
+static int LegacySurfH(struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *S)
+{
+    return ((S != NULL) && (S->Surface != NULL)) ? S->Surface->h : -1;
+}
+
+static void LegacyBlitMark(struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *Dst, struct _rect *DstRect,
+                           struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *Src, struct _rect *SrcRect)
+{
+    FILE *Log = LegacyBlitLogFile();
+
+    LegacyBlitCount++;
+
+    if ((Log == NULL) || (LegacyBlitCount > 300))
+    {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    fprintf(Log, "blit dst=%dx%d dstrect=(%d,%d,%d,%d) src=%dx%d srcrect=(%d,%d,%d,%d)\n",
+            LegacySurfW(Dst), LegacySurfH(Dst),
+            DstRect ? DstRect->left : -1, DstRect ? DstRect->top : -1,
+            DstRect ? DstRect->right : -1, DstRect ? DstRect->bottom : -1,
+            LegacySurfW(Src), LegacySurfH(Src),
+            SrcRect ? SrcRect->left : -1, SrcRect ? SrcRect->top : -1,
+            SrcRect ? SrcRect->right : -1, SrcRect ? SrcRect->bottom : -1);
+}
+
+static void LegacyLockMark(void)
+{
+    LegacyLockCount++;
+}
+
+static void LegacyInputPump(void);
+
+static void LegacyFrameEnd(void)
+{
+    LegacyInputPump();
+    FILE *Log = LegacyBlitLogFile();
+
+    if (Log == NULL)
+    {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    LegacyFrameNo++;
+    fprintf(Log, "frame %lu blits=%lu locks=%lu\n", LegacyFrameNo, LegacyBlitCount, LegacyLockCount);
+    fflush(Log);
+
+    LegacyBlitCount = 0;
+    LegacyLockCount = 0;
+}
+/* ---- legacy scripted input ------------------------------------------------
+ * LEGACY_INPUT="6000,click,120,450;9000,key,13" replays actions at the given
+ * millisecond offsets so a windowless run can walk the menus.
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+static void LegacyInputPump(void)
+{
+    static int State = 0;
+    static unsigned long DueMs[32];
+    static char Kind[32][8];
+    static int ArgA[32];
+    static int ArgB[32];
+    static unsigned char Done[32];
+    static int Count = 0;
+    static Uint32 Start = 0;
+
+    if (State == 0)
+    {
+        const char *Env = getenv("LEGACY_INPUT");
+
+        State = -1;
+
+        if ((Env != NULL) && (Env[0] != '\0'))
+        {
+            char Buffer[1024];
+            char *Entry;
+
+            snprintf(Buffer, sizeof(Buffer), "%s", Env);
+            Entry = Buffer;
+
+            while ((Entry != NULL) && (*Entry != '\0') && (Count < 32))
+            {
+                char *Next = strchr(Entry, ';');
+                unsigned long Ms = 0;
+                char KindBuf[8] = "";
+                int A = 0;
+                int B = 0;
+
+                if (Next != NULL)
+                {
+                    *Next = '\0';
+                    Next++;
+                }
+
+                if (sscanf(Entry, "%lu,%7[^,],%d,%d", &Ms, KindBuf, &A, &B) >= 3)
+                {
+                    DueMs[Count] = Ms;
+                    snprintf(Kind[Count], sizeof(Kind[Count]), "%s", KindBuf);
+                    ArgA[Count] = A;
+                    ArgB[Count] = B;
+                    Done[Count] = 0;
+                    Count++;
+                }
+
+                Entry = Next;
+            }
+
+            if (Count > 0)
+            {
+                State = 1;
+                eprintf("legacy-input: %d actions scheduled\n", Count);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    if (State != 1) return;
+    if (Start == 0) Start = SDL_GetTicks();
+
+    {
+        Uint32 Now = SDL_GetTicks() - Start;
+        int i;
+
+        for (i = 0; i < Count; i++)
+        {
+            if ((Done[i] == 0) && (Now >= (Uint32)DueMs[i]))
+            {
+                Done[i] = 1;
+
+                if (strcmp(Kind[i], "key") == 0)
+                {
+                    Patch_PushKeyEvent((uint32_t)ArgA[i]);
+                    eprintf("legacy-input: t=%lu key %d\n", (unsigned long)Now, ArgA[i]);
+                }
+                else if (strcmp(Kind[i], "click") == 0)
+                {
+                    Patch_PushMouseClick(ArgA[i], ArgB[i]);
+                    eprintf("legacy-input: t=%lu click %d,%d\n", (unsigned long)Now, ArgA[i], ArgB[i]);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+
+/* ---- legacy frame capture -------------------------------------------------
+ * Gives a windowless run eyes: LEGACY_SHOT_DIR=<dir> saves a BMP every
+ * LEGACY_SHOT_EVERY presents (default 60). Used to verify rendering and art
+ * packs without a desktop session.
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+static void LegacyScreenshot(SDL_Renderer *Renderer)
+{
+    static char Dir[256];
+    static int State = 0;
+    static unsigned long Calls = 0;
+    static unsigned long Every = 60;
+    SDL_Surface *Shot;
+    int Width, Height;
+    char Path[512];
+
+    if (State == 0)
+    {
+        const char *EnvDir = getenv("LEGACY_SHOT_DIR");
+        const char *EnvEvery = getenv("LEGACY_SHOT_EVERY");
+
+        State = -1;
+
+        if ((EnvDir != NULL) && (EnvDir[0] != '\0'))
+        {
+            snprintf(Dir, sizeof(Dir), "%s", EnvDir);
+            if (EnvEvery != NULL)
+            {
+                unsigned long Value = (unsigned long)strtoul(EnvEvery, NULL, 10);
+                if (Value > 0) Every = Value;
+            }
+            State = 1;
+            eprintf("legacy-shot: enabled dir=%s every=%lu\n", Dir, Every);
+        }
+    }
+
+    if ((State != 1) || (Renderer == NULL)) return;
+
+    Calls++;
+    if ((Calls % Every) != 0) return;
+
+    if (SDL_GetRendererOutputSize(Renderer, &Width, &Height) != 0) return;
+
+    Shot = SDL_CreateRGBSurfaceWithFormat(0, Width, Height, 32, SDL_PIXELFORMAT_ARGB8888);
+    if (Shot == NULL) return;
+
+    if (SDL_RenderReadPixels(Renderer, NULL, SDL_PIXELFORMAT_ARGB8888, Shot->pixels, Shot->pitch) == 0)
+    {
+        snprintf(Path, sizeof(Path), "%s/shot_%05lu.bmp", Dir, Calls);
+        SDL_SaveBMP(Shot, Path);
+    }
+
+    SDL_FreeSurface(Shot);
+}
