+/* ---- legacy post-process upscale (EPX / Scale2x) --------------------------
+ * The engine renders 640x480 no matter what. This takes that finished frame and
+ * doubles it with edge-aware interpolation instead of blocky nearest-neighbour.
+ * Wrapper only: no engine code involved.
+ *   LEGACY_UPSCALE=2x   enable
+ *   (unset / anything else) off, byte-for-byte original behaviour
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+static int LegacyUpscaleFactor(void)
+{
+    static int Factor = 0;
+
+    if (Factor == 0)
+    {
+        const char *Env = getenv("LEGACY_UPSCALE");
+
+        Factor = 1;
+
+        if ((Env != NULL) &&
+            ((strcmp(Env, "2x") == 0) || (strcmp(Env, "epx") == 0) || (strcmp(Env, "2") == 0)))
+        {
+            Factor = 2;
+            eprintf("legacy-upscale: EPX 2x enabled\n");
+        }
+    }
+
+    return Factor;
+}
+
+static uint32_t LegacyReadPixel(const uint8_t *p, int BytesPerPixel)
+{
+    if (BytesPerPixel == 1) return *p;
+    if (BytesPerPixel == 2) return *(const uint16_t *)p;
+    return *(const uint32_t *)p;
+}
+
+static void LegacyWritePixel(uint8_t *p, int BytesPerPixel, uint32_t Value)
+{
+    if (BytesPerPixel == 1) { *p = (uint8_t)Value; }
+    else if (BytesPerPixel == 2) { *(uint16_t *)p = (uint16_t)Value; }
+    else { *(uint32_t *)p = Value; }
+}
+
+static void LegacyUpscaleEPX2(const uint8_t *Src, int Width, int Height, int SrcPitch,
+                              uint8_t *Dst, int DstPitch, int BytesPerPixel)
+{
+    int x, y;
+
+    for (y = 0; y < Height; y++)
+    {
+        const uint8_t *Row = Src + (size_t)y * SrcPitch;
+        const uint8_t *UpRow = Src + (size_t)((y > 0) ? (y - 1) : y) * SrcPitch;
+        const uint8_t *DownRow = Src + (size_t)((y < Height - 1) ? (y + 1) : y) * SrcPitch;
+        uint8_t *OutTop = Dst + (size_t)(2 * y) * DstPitch;
+        uint8_t *OutBottom = OutTop + DstPitch;
+
+        for (x = 0; x < Width; x++)
+        {
+            int Left = (x > 0) ? (x - 1) : x;
+            int Right = (x < Width - 1) ? (x + 1) : x;
+            uint32_t P = LegacyReadPixel(Row + (size_t)x * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel);
+            uint32_t A = LegacyReadPixel(UpRow + (size_t)x * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel);
+            uint32_t D = LegacyReadPixel(DownRow + (size_t)x * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel);
+            uint32_t C = LegacyReadPixel(Row + (size_t)Left * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel);
+            uint32_t B = LegacyReadPixel(Row + (size_t)Right * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel);
+            uint32_t E0 = P, E1 = P, E2 = P, E3 = P;
+
+            if ((C == A) && (C != D) && (A != B)) E0 = A;
+            if ((A == B) && (A != C) && (B != D)) E1 = B;
+            if ((D == C) && (D != B) && (C != A)) E2 = C;
+            if ((B == D) && (B != A) && (D != C)) E3 = D;
+
+            LegacyWritePixel(OutTop + (size_t)(2 * x) * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel, E0);
+            LegacyWritePixel(OutTop + (size_t)(2 * x + 1) * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel, E1);
+            LegacyWritePixel(OutBottom + (size_t)(2 * x) * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel, E2);
+            LegacyWritePixel(OutBottom + (size_t)(2 * x + 1) * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel, E3);
+        }
+    }
+}
+

+/* ---- legacy blit instrumentation -----------------------------------------
+ * Answers one question: does the wrapper see the engine drawing each sprite?
+ * Set LEGACY_BLIT_LOG=<file path> and play. Every present writes one line:
+ *     frame <n> blits=<n> locks=<n>
+ * plus up to 300 individual blit lines carrying the rectangles.
+ * Many blits with small rectangles -> the wrapper sees per-sprite drawing.
+ * Few blits but many locks         -> the engine writes pixels itself.
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+static FILE *LegacyBlitLog = NULL;
+static int LegacyBlitLogState = 0;
+static unsigned long LegacyBlitCount = 0;
+static unsigned long LegacyLockCount = 0;
+static unsigned long LegacyFrameNo = 0;
+
+static FILE *LegacyBlitLogFile(void)
+{
+    if (LegacyBlitLogState == 0)
+    {
+        const char *Path = getenv("LEGACY_BLIT_LOG");
+
+        LegacyBlitLogState = -1;
+
+        if ((Path != NULL) && (Path[0] != '\0'))
+        {
+            LegacyBlitLog = fopen(Path, "w");
+
+            if (LegacyBlitLog != NULL)
+            {
+                LegacyBlitLogState = 1;
+                fprintf(LegacyBlitLog, "# frame blits locks\n");
+                fflush(LegacyBlitLog);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    return LegacyBlitLog;
+}
+
+static int LegacySurfW(struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *S)
+{
+    return ((S != NULL) && (S->Surface != NULL)) ? S->Surface->w : -1;
+}
+
+static int LegacySurfH(struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *S)
+{
+    return ((S != NULL) && (S->Surface != NULL)) ? S->Surface->h : -1;
+}
+
+/* ---- legacy surface dump --------------------------------------------------
+ * LEGACY_DUMP_SURFACES=<dir> saves the first N distinct source surfaces that get
+ * blitted, as BMPs named surf_<w>x<h>_<n>.bmp. The engine's own copy of each
+ * sprite, in the format it actually samples.
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+static void LegacyDumpSurface(struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *Src)
+{
+    static char Dir[256];
+    static int State = 0;
+    static struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *Seen[512];
+    static int SeenCount = 0;
+    static int Saved = 0;
+    char Path[512];
+    int i;
+
+    if (State == 0)
+    {
+        const char *Env = getenv("LEGACY_DUMP_SURFACES");
+
+        State = -1;
+
+        if ((Env != NULL) && (Env[0] != '\0'))
+        {
+            snprintf(Dir, sizeof(Dir), "%s", Env);
+            State = 1;
+            eprintf("legacy-dump: dumping blitted surfaces to %s\n", Dir);
+        }
+    }
+
+    if ((State != 1) || (Src == NULL) || (Src->Surface == NULL)) return;
+    if (Saved >= 200) return;
+
+    for (i = 0; i < SeenCount; i++)
+    {
+        if (Seen[i] == Src) return;
+    }
+
+    if (SeenCount < 512) Seen[SeenCount++] = Src;
+
+    snprintf(Path, sizeof(Path), "%s/surf_%dx%d_%03d.bmp", Dir, Src->Surface->w, Src->Surface->h, Saved);
+
+    {
+        static int Logged = 0;
+
+        if (SDL_SaveBMP(Src->Surface, Path) == 0)
+        {
+            Saved++;
+            if (Logged < 5)
+            {
+                Logged++;
+                eprintf("legacy-dump: %dx%d bpp=%d -> %s\n", Src->Surface->w, Src->Surface->h,
+                        Src->Surface->format->BytesPerPixel, Path);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+static void LegacyBlitMark(struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *Dst, struct _rect *DstRect,
+                           struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *Src, struct _rect *SrcRect)
+{
+    FILE *Log = LegacyBlitLogFile();
+
+    LegacyDumpSurface(Src);
+
+    LegacyBlitCount++;
+
+    if ((Log == NULL) || (LegacyBlitCount > 300))
+    {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    fprintf(Log, "blit dst=%dx%d dstrect=(%d,%d,%d,%d) src=%dx%d srcrect=(%d,%d,%d,%d)\n",
+            LegacySurfW(Dst), LegacySurfH(Dst),
+            DstRect ? DstRect->left : -1, DstRect ? DstRect->top : -1,
+            DstRect ? DstRect->right : -1, DstRect ? DstRect->bottom : -1,
+            LegacySurfW(Src), LegacySurfH(Src),
+            SrcRect ? SrcRect->left : -1, SrcRect ? SrcRect->top : -1,
+            SrcRect ? SrcRect->right : -1, SrcRect ? SrcRect->bottom : -1);
+}
+
+static void LegacyLockMark(void)
+{
+    LegacyLockCount++;
+}
+
+static void LegacyInputPump(void);
+
+static void LegacyFrameEnd(void)
+{
+    LegacyInputPump();
+    FILE *Log = LegacyBlitLogFile();
+
+    if (Log == NULL)
+    {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    LegacyFrameNo++;
+    fprintf(Log, "frame %lu blits=%lu locks=%lu\n", LegacyFrameNo, LegacyBlitCount, LegacyLockCount);
+    fflush(Log);
+
+    LegacyBlitCount = 0;
+    LegacyLockCount = 0;
+}
+/* ---- legacy scripted input ------------------------------------------------
+ * LEGACY_INPUT="6000,click,120,450;9000,key,13" replays actions at the given
+ * millisecond offsets so a windowless run can walk the menus.
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+static void LegacyInputPump(void)
+{
+    static int State = 0;
+    static unsigned long DueMs[32];
+    static char Kind[32][8];
+    static int ArgA[32];
+    static int ArgB[32];
+    static unsigned char Done[32];
+    static int Count = 0;
+    static Uint32 Start = 0;
+
+    if (State == 0)
+    {
+        const char *Env = getenv("LEGACY_INPUT");
+
+        State = -1;
+
+        if ((Env != NULL) && (Env[0] != '\0'))
+        {
+            char Buffer[1024];
+            char *Entry;
+
+            snprintf(Buffer, sizeof(Buffer), "%s", Env);
+            Entry = Buffer;
+
+            while ((Entry != NULL) && (*Entry != '\0') && (Count < 32))
+            {
+                char *Next = strchr(Entry, ';');
+                unsigned long Ms = 0;
+                char KindBuf[8] = "";
+                int A = 0;
+                int B = 0;
+
+                if (Next != NULL)
+                {
+                    *Next = '\0';
+                    Next++;
+                }
+
+                if (sscanf(Entry, "%lu,%7[^,],%d,%d", &Ms, KindBuf, &A, &B) >= 3)
+                {
+                    DueMs[Count] = Ms;
+                    snprintf(Kind[Count], sizeof(Kind[Count]), "%s", KindBuf);
+                    ArgA[Count] = A;
+                    ArgB[Count] = B;
+                    Done[Count] = 0;
+                    Count++;
+                }
+
+                Entry = Next;
+            }
+
+            if (Count > 0)
+            {
+                State = 1;
+                eprintf("legacy-input: %d actions scheduled\n", Count);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    if (State != 1) return;
+    if (Start == 0) Start = SDL_GetTicks();
+
+    {
+        Uint32 Now = SDL_GetTicks() - Start;
+        int i;
+
+        for (i = 0; i < Count; i++)
+        {
+            if ((Done[i] == 0) && (Now >= (Uint32)DueMs[i]))
+            {
+                Done[i] = 1;
+
+                if (strcmp(Kind[i], "key") == 0)
+                {
+                    Patch_PushKeyEvent((uint32_t)ArgA[i]);
+                    eprintf("legacy-input: t=%lu key %d\n", (unsigned long)Now, ArgA[i]);
+                }
+                else if (strcmp(Kind[i], "click") == 0)
+                {
+                    Patch_PushMouseClick(ArgA[i], ArgB[i]);
+                    eprintf("legacy-input: t=%lu click %d,%d\n", (unsigned long)Now, ArgA[i], ArgB[i]);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+
+/* ---- legacy frame capture -------------------------------------------------
+ * Gives a windowless run eyes: LEGACY_SHOT_DIR=<dir> saves a BMP every
+ * LEGACY_SHOT_EVERY presents (default 60). Used to verify rendering and art
+ * packs without a desktop session.
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+static void LegacyScreenshot(SDL_Renderer *Renderer)
+{
+    static char Dir[256];
+    static int State = 0;
+    static unsigned long Calls = 0;
+    static unsigned long Every = 60;
+    SDL_Surface *Shot;
+    int Width, Height;
+    char Path[512];
+
+    if (State == 0)
+    {
+        const char *EnvDir = getenv("LEGACY_SHOT_DIR");
+        const char *EnvEvery = getenv("LEGACY_SHOT_EVERY");
+
+        State = -1;
+
+        if ((EnvDir != NULL) && (EnvDir[0] != '\0'))
+        {
+            snprintf(Dir, sizeof(Dir), "%s", EnvDir);
+            if (EnvEvery != NULL)
+            {
+                unsigned long Value = (unsigned long)strtoul(EnvEvery, NULL, 10);
+                if (Value > 0) Every = Value;
+            }
+            State = 1;
+            eprintf("legacy-shot: enabled dir=%s every=%lu\n", Dir, Every);
+        }
+    }
+
+    if ((State != 1) || (Renderer == NULL)) return;
+
+    Calls++;
+    if ((Calls % Every) != 0) return;
+
+    if (SDL_GetRendererOutputSize(Renderer, &Width, &Height) != 0) return;
+
+    Shot = SDL_CreateRGBSurfaceWithFormat(0, Width, Height, 32, SDL_PIXELFORMAT_ARGB8888);
+    if (Shot == NULL) return;
+
+    if (SDL_RenderReadPixels(Renderer, NULL, SDL_PIXELFORMAT_ARGB8888, Shot->pixels, Shot->pitch) == 0)
+    {
+        snprintf(Path, sizeof(Path), "%s/shot_%05lu.bmp", Dir, Calls);
+        SDL_SaveBMP(Shot, Path);
+    }
+
+    SDL_FreeSurface(Shot);
+}

+/* ---- legacy post-process upscale (EPX / Scale2x) --------------------------
+ * The engine renders 640x480 no matter what. This takes that finished frame and
+ * doubles it with edge-aware interpolation instead of blocky nearest-neighbour.
+ * Wrapper only: no engine code involved.
+ *   LEGACY_UPSCALE=2x   enable
+ *   (unset / anything else) off, byte-for-byte original behaviour
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+static int LegacyUpscaleFactor(void)
+{
+    static int Factor = 0;
+
+    if (Factor == 0)
+    {
+        const char *Env = getenv("LEGACY_UPSCALE");
+
+        Factor = 1;
+
+        if ((Env != NULL) &&
+            ((strcmp(Env, "2x") == 0) || (strcmp(Env, "epx") == 0) || (strcmp(Env, "2") == 0)))
+        {
+            Factor = 2;
+            eprintf("legacy-upscale: EPX 2x enabled\n");
+        }
+    }
+
+    return Factor;
+}
+
+static uint32_t LegacyReadPixel(const uint8_t *p, int BytesPerPixel)
+{
+    if (BytesPerPixel == 1) return *p;
+    if (BytesPerPixel == 2) return *(const uint16_t *)p;
+    return *(const uint32_t *)p;
+}
+
+static void LegacyWritePixel(uint8_t *p, int BytesPerPixel, uint32_t Value)
+{
+    if (BytesPerPixel == 1) { *p = (uint8_t)Value; }
+    else if (BytesPerPixel == 2) { *(uint16_t *)p = (uint16_t)Value; }
+    else { *(uint32_t *)p = Value; }
+}
+
+static void LegacyUpscaleEPX2(const uint8_t *Src, int Width, int Height, int SrcPitch,
+                              uint8_t *Dst, int DstPitch, int BytesPerPixel)
+{
+    int x, y;
+
+    for (y = 0; y < Height; y++)
+    {
+        const uint8_t *Row = Src + (size_t)y * SrcPitch;
+        const uint8_t *UpRow = Src + (size_t)((y > 0) ? (y - 1) : y) * SrcPitch;
+        const uint8_t *DownRow = Src + (size_t)((y < Height - 1) ? (y + 1) : y) * SrcPitch;
+        uint8_t *OutTop = Dst + (size_t)(2 * y) * DstPitch;
+        uint8_t *OutBottom = OutTop + DstPitch;
+
+        for (x = 0; x < Width; x++)
+        {
+            int Left = (x > 0) ? (x - 1) : x;
+            int Right = (x < Width - 1) ? (x + 1) : x;
+            uint32_t P = LegacyReadPixel(Row + (size_t)x * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel);
+            uint32_t A = LegacyReadPixel(UpRow + (size_t)x * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel);
+            uint32_t D = LegacyReadPixel(DownRow + (size_t)x * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel);
+            uint32_t C = LegacyReadPixel(Row + (size_t)Left * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel);
+            uint32_t B = LegacyReadPixel(Row + (size_t)Right * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel);
+            uint32_t E0 = P, E1 = P, E2 = P, E3 = P;
+
+            if ((C == A) && (C != D) && (A != B)) E0 = A;
+            if ((A == B) && (A != C) && (B != D)) E1 = B;
+            if ((D == C) && (D != B) && (C != A)) E2 = C;
+            if ((B == D) && (B != A) && (D != C)) E3 = D;
+
+            LegacyWritePixel(OutTop + (size_t)(2 * x) * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel, E0);
+            LegacyWritePixel(OutTop + (size_t)(2 * x + 1) * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel, E1);
+            LegacyWritePixel(OutBottom + (size_t)(2 * x) * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel, E2);
+            LegacyWritePixel(OutBottom + (size_t)(2 * x + 1) * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel, E3);
+        }
+    }
+}
+

+/* ---- legacy blit instrumentation -----------------------------------------
+ * Answers one question: does the wrapper see the engine drawing each sprite?
+ * Set LEGACY_BLIT_LOG=<file path> and play. Every present writes one line:
+ *     frame <n> blits=<n> locks=<n>
+ * plus up to 300 individual blit lines carrying the rectangles.
+ * Many blits with small rectangles -> the wrapper sees per-sprite drawing.
+ * Few blits but many locks         -> the engine writes pixels itself.
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+static FILE *LegacyBlitLog = NULL;
+static int LegacyBlitLogState = 0;
+static unsigned long LegacyBlitCount = 0;
+static unsigned long LegacyLockCount = 0;
+static unsigned long LegacyFrameNo = 0;
+
+static FILE *LegacyBlitLogFile(void)
+{
+    if (LegacyBlitLogState == 0)
+    {
+        const char *Path = getenv("LEGACY_BLIT_LOG");
+
+        LegacyBlitLogState = -1;
+
+        if ((Path != NULL) && (Path[0] != '\0'))
+        {
+            LegacyBlitLog = fopen(Path, "w");
+
+            if (LegacyBlitLog != NULL)
+            {
+                LegacyBlitLogState = 1;
+                fprintf(LegacyBlitLog, "# frame blits locks\n");
+                fflush(LegacyBlitLog);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    return LegacyBlitLog;
+}
+
+static int LegacySurfW(struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *S)
+{
+    return ((S != NULL) && (S->Surface != NULL)) ? S->Surface->w : -1;
+}
+
+static int LegacySurfH(struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *S)
+{
+    return ((S != NULL) && (S->Surface != NULL)) ? S->Surface->h : -1;
+}
+
+/* ---- legacy surface dump --------------------------------------------------
+ * LEGACY_DUMP_SURFACES=<dir> saves the first N distinct source surfaces that get
+ * blitted, as BMPs named surf_<w>x<h>_<n>.bmp. The engine's own copy of each
+ * sprite, in the format it actually samples.
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+/* ---- legacy art override --------------------------------------------------
+ * LEGACY_ART=<bmp path> replaces the contents of any source surface whose size
+ * matches the image, the first time it is blitted. Proof-of-concept for packs:
+ * the game displays the supplied artwork instead of its own.
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+static void LegacyArtOverride(struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *Src)
+{
+    static char Path[256];
+    static int State = 0;
+    static SDL_Surface *Art = NULL;
+    static struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *Done[64];
+    static int DoneCount = 0;
+    int i;
+
+    if (State == 0)
+    {
+        const char *Env = getenv("LEGACY_ART");
+
+        State = -1;
+
+        if ((Env != NULL) && (Env[0] != '\0'))
+        {
+            snprintf(Path, sizeof(Path), "%s", Env);
+            Art = SDL_LoadBMP(Path);
+
+            if (Art != NULL)
+            {
+                State = 1;
+                eprintf("legacy-art: loaded %s (%dx%d)\n", Path, Art->w, Art->h);
+            }
+            else
+            {
+                eprintf("legacy-art: cannot load %s: %s\n", Path, SDL_GetError());
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    if ((State != 1) || (Src == NULL) || (Src->Surface == NULL)) return;
+    if ((Src->Surface->w != Art->w) || (Src->Surface->h != Art->h)) return;
+
+    for (i = 0; i < DoneCount; i++)
+    {
+        if (Done[i] == Src) return;
+    }
+
+    {
+        SDL_Surface *Converted = SDL_ConvertSurface(Art, Src->Surface->format, 0);
+
+        if (Converted != NULL)
+        {
+            SDL_BlitSurface(Converted, NULL, Src->Surface, NULL);
+            SDL_FreeSurface(Converted);
+
+            if (DoneCount < 64) Done[DoneCount++] = Src;
+
+            eprintf("legacy-art: replaced surface %dx%d\n", Src->Surface->w, Src->Surface->h);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+static void LegacyDumpSurface(struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *Src)
+{
+    static char Dir[256];
+    static int State = 0;
+    static struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *Seen[512];
+    static int SeenCount = 0;
+    static int Saved = 0;
+    char Path[512];
+    int i;
+
+    if (State == 0)
+    {
+        const char *Env = getenv("LEGACY_DUMP_SURFACES");
+
+        State = -1;
+
+        if ((Env != NULL) && (Env[0] != '\0'))
+        {
+            snprintf(Dir, sizeof(Dir), "%s", Env);
+            State = 1;
+            eprintf("legacy-dump: dumping blitted surfaces to %s\n", Dir);
+        }
+    }
+
+    if ((State != 1) || (Src == NULL) || (Src->Surface == NULL)) return;
+    if (Saved >= 200) return;
+
+    for (i = 0; i < SeenCount; i++)
+    {
+        if (Seen[i] == Src) return;
+    }
+
+    if (SeenCount < 512) Seen[SeenCount++] = Src;
+
+    snprintf(Path, sizeof(Path), "%s/surf_%dx%d_%03d.bmp", Dir, Src->Surface->w, Src->Surface->h, Saved);
+
+    {
+        static int Logged = 0;
+
+        if (SDL_SaveBMP(Src->Surface, Path) == 0)
+        {
+            Saved++;
+            if (Logged < 5)
+            {
+                Logged++;
+                eprintf("legacy-dump: %dx%d bpp=%d -> %s\n", Src->Surface->w, Src->Surface->h,
+                        Src->Surface->format->BytesPerPixel, Path);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+static void LegacyBlitMark(struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *Dst, struct _rect *DstRect,
+                           struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *Src, struct _rect *SrcRect)
+{
+    FILE *Log = LegacyBlitLogFile();
+
+    LegacyArtOverride(Src);
+    LegacyDumpSurface(Src);
+
+    LegacyBlitCount++;
+
+    if ((Log == NULL) || (LegacyBlitCount > 300))
+    {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    fprintf(Log, "blit dst=%dx%d dstrect=(%d,%d,%d,%d) src=%dx%d srcrect=(%d,%d,%d,%d)\n",
+            LegacySurfW(Dst), LegacySurfH(Dst),
+            DstRect ? DstRect->left : -1, DstRect ? DstRect->top : -1,
+            DstRect ? DstRect->right : -1, DstRect ? DstRect->bottom : -1,
+            LegacySurfW(Src), LegacySurfH(Src),
+            SrcRect ? SrcRect->left : -1, SrcRect ? SrcRect->top : -1,
+            SrcRect ? SrcRect->right : -1, SrcRect ? SrcRect->bottom : -1);
+}
+
+static void LegacyLockMark(void)
+{
+    LegacyLockCount++;
+}
+
+static void LegacyInputPump(void);
+
+static void LegacyFrameEnd(void)
+{
+    LegacyInputPump();
+    FILE *Log = LegacyBlitLogFile();
+
+    if (Log == NULL)
+    {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    LegacyFrameNo++;
+    fprintf(Log, "frame %lu blits=%lu locks=%lu\n", LegacyFrameNo, LegacyBlitCount, LegacyLockCount);
+    fflush(Log);
+
+    LegacyBlitCount = 0;
+    LegacyLockCount = 0;
+}
+/* ---- legacy scripted input ------------------------------------------------
+ * LEGACY_INPUT="6000,click,120,450;9000,key,13" replays actions at the given
+ * millisecond offsets so a windowless run can walk the menus.
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+static void LegacyInputPump(void)
+{
+    static int State = 0;
+    static unsigned long DueMs[32];
+    static char Kind[32][8];
+    static int ArgA[32];
+    static int ArgB[32];
+    static unsigned char Done[32];
+    static int Count = 0;
+    static Uint32 Start = 0;
+
+    if (State == 0)
+    {
+        const char *Env = getenv("LEGACY_INPUT");
+
+        State = -1;
+
+        if ((Env != NULL) && (Env[0] != '\0'))
+        {
+            char Buffer[1024];
+            char *Entry;
+
+            snprintf(Buffer, sizeof(Buffer), "%s", Env);
+            Entry = Buffer;
+
+            while ((Entry != NULL) && (*Entry != '\0') && (Count < 32))
+            {
+                char *Next = strchr(Entry, ';');
+                unsigned long Ms = 0;
+                char KindBuf[8] = "";
+                int A = 0;
+                int B = 0;
+
+                if (Next != NULL)
+                {
+                    *Next = '\0';
+                    Next++;
+                }
+
+                if (sscanf(Entry, "%lu,%7[^,],%d,%d", &Ms, KindBuf, &A, &B) >= 3)
+                {
+                    DueMs[Count] = Ms;
+                    snprintf(Kind[Count], sizeof(Kind[Count]), "%s", KindBuf);
+                    ArgA[Count] = A;
+                    ArgB[Count] = B;
+                    Done[Count] = 0;
+                    Count++;
+                }
+
+                Entry = Next;
+            }
+
+            if (Count > 0)
+            {
+                State = 1;
+                eprintf("legacy-input: %d actions scheduled\n", Count);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    if (State != 1) return;
+    if (Start == 0) Start = SDL_GetTicks();
+
+    {
+        Uint32 Now = SDL_GetTicks() - Start;
+        int i;
+
+        for (i = 0; i < Count; i++)
+        {
+            if ((Done[i] == 0) && (Now >= (Uint32)DueMs[i]))
+            {
+                Done[i] = 1;
+
+                if (strcmp(Kind[i], "key") == 0)
+                {
+                    Patch_PushKeyEvent((uint32_t)ArgA[i]);
+                    eprintf("legacy-input: t=%lu key %d\n", (unsigned long)Now, ArgA[i]);
+                }
+                else if (strcmp(Kind[i], "click") == 0)
+                {
+                    Patch_PushMouseClick(ArgA[i], ArgB[i]);
+                    eprintf("legacy-input: t=%lu click %d,%d\n", (unsigned long)Now, ArgA[i], ArgB[i]);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+
+/* ---- legacy frame capture -------------------------------------------------
+ * Gives a windowless run eyes: LEGACY_SHOT_DIR=<dir> saves a BMP every
+ * LEGACY_SHOT_EVERY presents (default 60). Used to verify rendering and art
+ * packs without a desktop session.
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+static void LegacyScreenshot(SDL_Renderer *Renderer)
+{
+    static char Dir[256];
+    static int State = 0;
+    static unsigned long Calls = 0;
+    static unsigned long Every = 60;
+    SDL_Surface *Shot;
+    int Width, Height;
+    char Path[512];
+
+    if (State == 0)
+    {
+        const char *EnvDir = getenv("LEGACY_SHOT_DIR");
+        const char *EnvEvery = getenv("LEGACY_SHOT_EVERY");
+
+        State = -1;
+
+        if ((EnvDir != NULL) && (EnvDir[0] != '\0'))
+        {
+            snprintf(Dir, sizeof(Dir), "%s", EnvDir);
+            if (EnvEvery != NULL)
+            {
+                unsigned long Value = (unsigned long)strtoul(EnvEvery, NULL, 10);
+                if (Value > 0) Every = Value;
+            }
+            State = 1;
+            eprintf("legacy-shot: enabled dir=%s every=%lu\n", Dir, Every);
+        }
+    }
+
+    if ((State != 1) || (Renderer == NULL)) return;
+
+    Calls++;
+    if ((Calls % Every) != 0) return;
+
+    if (SDL_GetRendererOutputSize(Renderer, &Width, &Height) != 0) return;
+
+    Shot = SDL_CreateRGBSurfaceWithFormat(0, Width, Height, 32, SDL_PIXELFORMAT_ARGB8888);
+    if (Shot == NULL) return;
+
+    if (SDL_RenderReadPixels(Renderer, NULL, SDL_PIXELFORMAT_ARGB8888, Shot->pixels, Shot->pitch) == 0)
+    {
+        snprintf(Path, sizeof(Path), "%s/shot_%05lu.bmp", Dir, Calls);
+        SDL_SaveBMP(Shot, Path);
+    }
+
+    SDL_FreeSurface(Shot);
+}

+/* ---- legacy post-process upscale (EPX / Scale2x) --------------------------
+ * The engine renders 640x480 no matter what. This takes that finished frame and
+ * doubles it with edge-aware interpolation instead of blocky nearest-neighbour.
+ * Wrapper only: no engine code involved.
+ *   LEGACY_UPSCALE=2x   enable
+ *   (unset / anything else) off, byte-for-byte original behaviour
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+static int LegacyUpscaleFactor(void)
+{
+    static int Factor = 0;
+
+    if (Factor == 0)
+    {
+        const char *Env = getenv("LEGACY_UPSCALE");
+
+        Factor = 1;
+
+        if ((Env != NULL) &&
+            ((strcmp(Env, "2x") == 0) || (strcmp(Env, "epx") == 0) || (strcmp(Env, "2") == 0)))
+        {
+            Factor = 2;
+            eprintf("legacy-upscale: EPX 2x enabled\n");
+        }
+    }
+
+    return Factor;
+}
+
+static uint32_t LegacyReadPixel(const uint8_t *p, int BytesPerPixel)
+{
+    if (BytesPerPixel == 1) return *p;
+    if (BytesPerPixel == 2) return *(const uint16_t *)p;
+    return *(const uint32_t *)p;
+}
+
+static void LegacyWritePixel(uint8_t *p, int BytesPerPixel, uint32_t Value)
+{
+    if (BytesPerPixel == 1) { *p = (uint8_t)Value; }
+    else if (BytesPerPixel == 2) { *(uint16_t *)p = (uint16_t)Value; }
+    else { *(uint32_t *)p = Value; }
+}
+
+static void LegacyUpscaleEPX2(const uint8_t *Src, int Width, int Height, int SrcPitch,
+                              uint8_t *Dst, int DstPitch, int BytesPerPixel)
+{
+    int x, y;
+
+    for (y = 0; y < Height; y++)
+    {
+        const uint8_t *Row = Src + (size_t)y * SrcPitch;
+        const uint8_t *UpRow = Src + (size_t)((y > 0) ? (y - 1) : y) * SrcPitch;
+        const uint8_t *DownRow = Src + (size_t)((y < Height - 1) ? (y + 1) : y) * SrcPitch;
+        uint8_t *OutTop = Dst + (size_t)(2 * y) * DstPitch;
+        uint8_t *OutBottom = OutTop + DstPitch;
+
+        for (x = 0; x < Width; x++)
+        {
+            int Left = (x > 0) ? (x - 1) : x;
+            int Right = (x < Width - 1) ? (x + 1) : x;
+            uint32_t P = LegacyReadPixel(Row + (size_t)x * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel);
+            uint32_t A = LegacyReadPixel(UpRow + (size_t)x * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel);
+            uint32_t D = LegacyReadPixel(DownRow + (size_t)x * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel);
+            uint32_t C = LegacyReadPixel(Row + (size_t)Left * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel);
+            uint32_t B = LegacyReadPixel(Row + (size_t)Right * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel);
+            uint32_t E0 = P, E1 = P, E2 = P, E3 = P;
+
+            if ((C == A) && (C != D) && (A != B)) E0 = A;
+            if ((A == B) && (A != C) && (B != D)) E1 = B;
+            if ((D == C) && (D != B) && (C != A)) E2 = C;
+            if ((B == D) && (B != A) && (D != C)) E3 = D;
+
+            LegacyWritePixel(OutTop + (size_t)(2 * x) * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel, E0);
+            LegacyWritePixel(OutTop + (size_t)(2 * x + 1) * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel, E1);
+            LegacyWritePixel(OutBottom + (size_t)(2 * x) * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel, E2);
+            LegacyWritePixel(OutBottom + (size_t)(2 * x + 1) * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel, E3);
+        }
+    }
+}
+

+    FILE *PackFile;         // legacy pack override (blob level), NULL = normal
+    uint32_t PackSize;      // size of the override blob
 } RecordInfo;
 
+/* --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+ * Legacy pack override (blob level).
+ *
+ * Enabled by setting LEGACY_PACK_DIR. A record whose key has a file at
+ *   <dir>/blobs/<key>.bin      (8 or 1..8 lowercase hex digits, no 0x)
+ * is read from that file instead of the database, with the pack file's own
+ * size used for RecordGetSize. Anything absent falls through to the archive.
+ *
+ * This is the v0 override point from port/docs/pack_format.md: whole records
+ * only, no decompiled code required. Set LEGACY_PACK_TRACE=1 to log every key
+ * the game opens, which is how you discover keys to override.
+ * -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+static char LegacyPackDir[256];
+static int  LegacyPackState = 0;   /* 0 = not initialised, 1 = enabled, -1 = off */
+
+static void LegacyPackInit(void)
+{
+    const char *env = getenv("LEGACY_PACK_DIR");
+    LegacyPackState = -1;
+    if (env == NULL || env[0] == '\0') return;
+
+    /* tolerate quoting and a trailing separator: set LEGACY_PACK_DIR="D:\packs\" */
+    {
+        const char *src = env;
+        size_t n;
+
+        while (*src == '"' || *src == '\'') src++;
+        snprintf(LegacyPackDir, sizeof(LegacyPackDir), "%s", src);
+        n = strlen(LegacyPackDir);
+        while (n > 0 && (LegacyPackDir[n - 1] == '"' || LegacyPackDir[n - 1] == '\'' ||
+                         LegacyPackDir[n - 1] == '\\' || LegacyPackDir[n - 1] == '/'))
+            LegacyPackDir[--n] = '\0';
+    }
+    LegacyPackState = 1;
+    eprintf("legacy-pack: enabled, dir=%s\n", LegacyPackDir);
+}
+
+static int LegacyPackTrace(void)
+{
+    const char *env = getenv("LEGACY_PACK_TRACE");
+    return (env != NULL && env[0] != '\0' && env[0] != '0');
+}
+
+static const char *LegacyPackDumpDir(void)
+{
+    static char DumpDir[256];
+
+    if (DumpDir[0] != '\0') return DumpDir;
+
+    {
+        const char *env = getenv("LEGACY_PACK_DUMP");
+        const char *src;
+        size_t n;
+
+        if (env == NULL || env[0] == '\0') return NULL;
+
+        src = env;
+        while (*src == '"' || *src == '\'') src++;
+        snprintf(DumpDir, sizeof(DumpDir), "%s", src);
+        n = strlen(DumpDir);
+        while (n > 0 && (DumpDir[n - 1] == '"' || DumpDir[n - 1] == '\'' ||
+                         DumpDir[n - 1] == '\\' || DumpDir[n - 1] == '/'))
+            DumpDir[--n] = '\0';
+        if (DumpDir[0] == '\0') return NULL;
+        eprintf("legacy-pack: dump enabled, dir=%s\n", DumpDir);
+    }
+    return DumpDir;
+}
+
+/* Read a record once, write it out as <dir>/<key>.bin, then rewind it for the caller.
+ * Only runs when LEGACY_PACK_DUMP is set; this is how pack authors obtain the
+ * originals to build a pack from. */
+static void LegacyPackDumpRecord(int32_t RecordHandle, uint32_t RecordKey, uint32_t Size)
+{
+    const char *Dir = LegacyPackDumpDir();
+    char path[512];
+    uint8_t *Buffer;
+    FILE *Out;
+    uint32_t Read;
+
+    if (Dir == NULL || Size == 0) return;
+
+    Buffer = malloc(Size);
+    if (Buffer == NULL) return;
+
+    Read = RecordRead(RecordHandle, Buffer, Size);
+    RecordSeek(RecordHandle, 0, 0);
+
+    snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "%s/%08x.bin", Dir, RecordKey);
+    Out = fopen(path, "wb");
+    if (Out != NULL)
+    {
+        if (Read > 0) fwrite(Buffer, 1, Read, Out);
+        fclose(Out);
+        eprintf("legacy-pack: dumped key 0x%x (%u bytes) -> %s\n", RecordKey, Read, path);
+    }
+    else
+    {
+        eprintf("legacy-pack: dump write failed %s (errno %d)\n", path, errno);
+    }
+
+    free(Buffer);
+}
+
+static FILE *LegacyPackOpenBlob(uint32_t RecordKey, uint32_t *Size)
+{
+    char path[512];
+    FILE *File;
+    long Length;
+
+    if (LegacyPackState == 0) LegacyPackInit();
+    if (LegacyPackState != 1) return NULL;
+
+    snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "%s/blobs/%08x.bin", LegacyPackDir, RecordKey);
+    File = fopen(path, "rb");
+    if (File == NULL)
+    {
+        int FirstError = errno;
+        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "%s/blobs/%x.bin", LegacyPackDir, RecordKey);
+        File = fopen(path, "rb");
+        if (File == NULL)
+        {
+            if (LegacyPackTrace())
+                eprintf("legacy-pack: miss %s (errno %d)\n", path, FirstError);
+            return NULL;
+        }
+    }
+
+    if (fseek(File, 0, SEEK_END) != 0) { eprintf("legacy-pack: seek failed %s (errno %d)\n", path, errno); fclose(File); return NULL; }
+    Length = ftell(File);
+    if (Length < 0) { eprintf("legacy-pack: tell failed %s\n", path); fclose(File); return NULL; }
+    fseek(File, 0, SEEK_SET);
+
+    *Size = (uint32_t)Length;
+    eprintf("legacy-pack: override key 0x%x (%u bytes) <- %s\n", RecordKey, *Size, path);
+    return File;
+}
+

+    event.type = SDL_MOUSEBUTTONDOWN;
+    event.button.button = SDL_BUTTON_LEFT;
+    event.button.state = SDL_PRESSED;
+    event.button.clicks = 1;
+    event.button.x = x;
+    event.button.y = y;
+    SDL_PushEvent(&event);
+
+    memset(&event, 0, sizeof(event));
+    event.type = SDL_MOUSEBUTTONUP;
+    event.button.button = SDL_BUTTON_LEFT;
+    event.button.state = SDL_RELEASED;
+    event.button.clicks = 1;
+    event.button.x = x;
+    event.button.y = y;
+    SDL_PushEvent(&event);
+}

+/* ---- legacy blit instrumentation -----------------------------------------
+ * Answers one question: does the wrapper see the engine drawing each sprite?
+ * Set LEGACY_BLIT_LOG=<file path> and play. Every present writes one line:
+ *     frame <n> blits=<n> locks=<n>
+ * plus up to 300 individual blit lines carrying the rectangles.
+ * Many blits with small rectangles -> the wrapper sees per-sprite drawing.
+ * Few blits but many locks         -> the engine writes pixels itself.
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+static FILE *LegacyBlitLog = NULL;
+static int LegacyBlitLogState = 0;
+static unsigned long LegacyBlitCount = 0;
+static unsigned long LegacyLockCount = 0;
+static unsigned long LegacyFrameNo = 0;
+
+static FILE *LegacyBlitLogFile(void)
+{
+    if (LegacyBlitLogState == 0)
+    {
+        const char *Path = getenv("LEGACY_BLIT_LOG");
+
+        LegacyBlitLogState = -1;
+
+        if ((Path != NULL) && (Path[0] != '\0'))
+        {
+            LegacyBlitLog = fopen(Path, "w");
+
+            if (LegacyBlitLog != NULL)
+            {
+                LegacyBlitLogState = 1;
+                fprintf(LegacyBlitLog, "# frame blits locks\n");
+                fflush(LegacyBlitLog);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    return LegacyBlitLog;
+}
+
+static int LegacySurfW(struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *S)
+{
+    return ((S != NULL) && (S->Surface != NULL)) ? S->Surface->w : -1;
+}
+
+static int LegacySurfH(struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *S)
+{
+    return ((S != NULL) && (S->Surface != NULL)) ? S->Surface->h : -1;
+}
+
+/* ---- legacy surface dump --------------------------------------------------
+ * LEGACY_DUMP_SURFACES=<dir> saves the first N distinct source surfaces that get
+ * blitted, as BMPs named surf_<w>x<h>_<n>.bmp. The engine's own copy of each
+ * sprite, in the format it actually samples.
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+/* ---- legacy art override --------------------------------------------------
+ * LEGACY_ART=<bmp path> replaces the contents of any source surface whose size
+ * matches the image, the first time it is blitted. Proof-of-concept for packs:
+ * the game displays the supplied artwork instead of its own.
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+/* ---- legacy caller trace ---------------------------------------------------
+ * LEGACY_CALLERS=1 logs the engine-side return address of the first few Blt/Lock/
+ * Flip calls. Mapped against Septerra.map (/MAP in the build) that names the
+ * original function responsible - the bridge from runtime behaviour to Ghidra.
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+/* ---- legacy backtrace ------------------------------------------------------
+ * LEGACY_BT=1 dumps the call stack of the first Lock calls. Resolved against
+ * SR-Septerra.map (which carries the original addresses) this names the engine
+ * function that paints: the bridge from runtime to Ghidra.
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+/* ---- legacy surface-global finder -----------------------------------------
+ * LEGACY_FIND_GLOBAL=1 scans the engine's .data for a stored pointer equal to the
+ * surface handed to Lock. That global is how the engine reaches its screen, and
+ * therefore the entry point for finding the paint routine.
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+static void LegacyFindSurfaceGlobal(void *Surface)
+{
+    static int Done = 0;
+    unsigned long Target = (unsigned long)Surface;
+    unsigned long *Slot;
+    int Hits = 0;
+
+    if (Done) return;
+    if (getenv("LEGACY_FIND_GLOBAL") == NULL) return;
+
+    Done = 1;
+
+    for (Slot = (unsigned long *)0x46C000UL; Slot < (unsigned long *)0x4AAFB8UL; Slot++)
+    {
+        if (*Slot == Target)
+        {
+            eprintf("legacy-global: surface %p stored at %p\n", Surface, (void *)Slot);
+
+            if (++Hits >= 40) break;
+        }
+    }
+
+    if (Hits == 0) eprintf("legacy-global: surface %p not found in .data\n", Surface);
+}
+
+static void LegacyBacktrace(const char *What)
+{
+    static int Calls = 0;
+    unsigned long *Frame = 0;
+    int i;
+
+    if (getenv("LEGACY_BT") == NULL) return;
+    if (Calls >= 2) return;
+
+    Calls++;
+
+    __asm { mov Frame, ebp }
+
+    for (i = 0; (i < 12) && (Frame != 0); i++)
+    {
+        unsigned long Next = Frame[0];
+        unsigned long Ret = Frame[1];
+
+        if (Ret != 0) eprintf("legacy-bt: %s %d %p\n", What, i, (void *)Ret);
+
+        if ((Next <= (unsigned long)Frame) || (Next > 0x7fffffffUL)) break;
+
+        Frame = (unsigned long *)Next;
+    }
+}
+
+static void LegacyCallerTrace(const char *What)
+{
+    static int Calls = 0;
+
+    if ((getenv("LEGACY_CALLERS") == NULL) || (Calls >= 30)) return;
+
+    Calls++;
+    eprintf("legacy-caller: %s from %p\n", What, _ReturnAddress());
+}

+static void LegacyArtOverride(struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *Src)
+{
+    static char Path[256];
+    static int State = 0;
+    static SDL_Surface *Art = NULL;
+    static struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *Done[64];
+    static int DoneCount = 0;
+    int i;
+
+    if (State == 0)
+    {
+        const char *Env = getenv("LEGACY_ART");
+
+        State = -1;
+
+        if ((Env != NULL) && (Env[0] != '\0'))
+        {
+            snprintf(Path, sizeof(Path), "%s", Env);
+            Art = SDL_LoadBMP(Path);
+
+            if (Art != NULL)
+            {
+                State = 1;
+                eprintf("legacy-art: loaded %s (%dx%d)\n", Path, Art->w, Art->h);
+            }
+            else
+            {
+                eprintf("legacy-art: cannot load %s: %s\n", Path, SDL_GetError());
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    if ((State != 1) || (Src == NULL) || (Src->Surface == NULL)) return;
+    if ((Src->Surface->w != Art->w) || (Src->Surface->h != Art->h)) return;
+
+    for (i = 0; i < DoneCount; i++)
+    {
+        if (Done[i] == Src) return;
+    }
+
+    {
+        SDL_Surface *Converted = SDL_ConvertSurface(Art, Src->Surface->format, 0);
+
+        if (Converted != NULL)
+        {
+            SDL_BlitSurface(Converted, NULL, Src->Surface, NULL);
+            SDL_FreeSurface(Converted);
+
+            if (DoneCount < 64) Done[DoneCount++] = Src;
+
+            eprintf("legacy-art: replaced surface %dx%d\n", Src->Surface->w, Src->Surface->h);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+static void LegacyDumpSurface(struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *Src)
+{
+    static char Dir[256];
+    static int State = 0;
+    static struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *Seen[512];
+    static int SeenCount = 0;
+    static int Saved = 0;
+    char Path[512];
+    int i;
+
+    if (State == 0)
+    {
+        const char *Env = getenv("LEGACY_DUMP_SURFACES");
+
+        State = -1;
+
+        if ((Env != NULL) && (Env[0] != '\0'))
+        {
+            snprintf(Dir, sizeof(Dir), "%s", Env);
+            State = 1;
+            eprintf("legacy-dump: dumping blitted surfaces to %s\n", Dir);
+        }
+    }
+
+    if ((State != 1) || (Src == NULL) || (Src->Surface == NULL)) return;
+    if (Saved >= 200) return;
+
+    for (i = 0; i < SeenCount; i++)
+    {
+        if (Seen[i] == Src) return;
+    }
+
+    if (SeenCount < 512) Seen[SeenCount++] = Src;
+
+    snprintf(Path, sizeof(Path), "%s/surf_%dx%d_%03d.bmp", Dir, Src->Surface->w, Src->Surface->h, Saved);
+
+    {
+        static int Logged = 0;
+
+        if (SDL_SaveBMP(Src->Surface, Path) == 0)
+        {
+            Saved++;
+            if (Logged < 5)
+            {
+                Logged++;
+                eprintf("legacy-dump: %dx%d bpp=%d -> %s\n", Src->Surface->w, Src->Surface->h,
+                        Src->Surface->format->BytesPerPixel, Path);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+/* ---- legacy image struct dump --------------------------------------------
+ * LEGACY_IMAGE_DIR=<dir> dumps the image structure the engine built from an
+ * AM04 blob: the eight relocated section pointers, plus raw copies of the
+ * header and the sections the blitter reads. Reads the global at 0x484F74
+ * (original DAT_004ae9a0) - the image the main view draws with.
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+/* ---- legacy image struct scan --------------------------------------------
+ * Instead of trusting one global address, look through the engine's .data for
+ * slots pointing at something shaped like a loaded AM04 image: a key in dword 0,
+ * a small type in dword 1, and a section pointer at +0x18 that lands inside the
+ * same allocation. LEGACY_FIND_IMAGE=1.
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+/* ---- legacy image cache dump ---------------------------------------------
+ * The engine caches loaded images in a table at 0x4865bc: {pointer, key, refs}
+ * triples, capacity in 0x4865c0. Enumerating it gives the true list of loaded
+ * images with their keys, instead of guessing at .data slots.
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+/* ---- legacy draw slots ---------------------------------------------------
+ * The engine keeps its working images in eleven globals loaded once at startup
+ * (FUN_0040f800). Reading them directly shows which images the scene path uses
+ * and whether their pixel pool (+0x40) is populated. LEGACY_SLOTS=1.
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+static void LegacyDumpSlots(void)
+{
+    static int Done = 0;
+    const unsigned long Slots[11] = {
+        0x485F5CUL, 0x485F60UL, 0x485F64UL, 0x485F68UL, 0x485F6CUL, 0x485F70UL,
+        0x485F74UL, 0x485F78UL, 0x485F7CUL, 0x485F80UL, 0x485F84UL
+    };
+    int i;
+
+    if (Done || (getenv("LEGACY_SLOTS") == NULL)) return;
+    if (SDL_GetTicks() < 25000) return;
+
+    Done = 1;
+
+    for (i = 0; i < 11; i++)
+    {
+        unsigned long Ptr = 0, Key = 0, D[8];
+        const int Offs[8] = { 0x08, 0x10, 0x18, 0x20, 0x28, 0x30, 0x38, 0x40 };
+        int k;
+
+        __try { Ptr = *(unsigned long *)Slots[i]; }
+        __except (1) { continue; }
+
+        if (Ptr == 0)
+        {
+            eprintf("legacy-slot: %d @%08x empty\n", i, (unsigned)Slots[i]);
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        __try { Key = *(unsigned long *)Ptr; }
+        __except (1) { continue; }
+
+        for (k = 0; k < 8; k++)
+        {
+            __try { D[k] = *(unsigned long *)(Ptr + Offs[k]) - Ptr; }
+            __except (1) { D[k] = 0xdeadbeefUL; }
+        }
+
+        eprintf("legacy-slot: %d ptr=%08x key=%08x d28=%08x d30=%08x d38=%08x d40=%08x\n",
+                i, (unsigned)Ptr, (unsigned)Key, (unsigned)D[4], (unsigned)D[5],
+                (unsigned)D[6], (unsigned)D[7]);
+    }
+}
+
+static void LegacyDumpImageCache(void)
+{
+    static int Done = 0;
+    unsigned long *Table;
+    int Cap, i, Hits = 0;
+    FILE *F;
+    char Path[512];
+
+    if (Done || (getenv("LEGACY_CACHE_DUMP") == NULL)) return;
+    if (SDL_GetTicks() < 25000) return;
+
+    Done = 1;
+
+    __try
+    {
+        Table = *(unsigned long **)0x4865BCUL;
+        Cap = (int)*(unsigned long *)0x4865C0UL;
+    }
+    __except (1)
+    {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    eprintf("legacy-cache: probe table=%p cap=%d\n", (void *)Table, Cap);
+
+    if ((Table == NULL) || (Cap <= 0) || (Cap > 4096)) return;
+
+    snprintf(Path, sizeof(Path), "%s/cache.txt", getenv("LEGACY_CACHE_DUMP"));
+    F = fopen(Path, "w");
+
+    if (F == NULL) return;
+
+    for (i = 0; i < Cap; i++)
+    {
+        unsigned long Ptr, Key, Refs;
+
+        __try
+        {
+            Ptr = Table[i * 3 + 0];
+            Key = Table[i * 3 + 1];
+            Refs = Table[i * 3 + 2];
+        }
+        __except (1)
+        {
+            break;
+        }
+
+        if (Ptr == 0) continue;
+
+        {
+            unsigned long D[8];
+            const int Offs[8] = { 0x08, 0x10, 0x18, 0x20, 0x28, 0x30, 0x38, 0x40 };
+            int k;
+
+            for (k = 0; k < 8; k++)
+            {
+                __try { D[k] = *(unsigned long *)(Ptr + Offs[k]) - Ptr; }
+                __except (1) { D[k] = 0xdeadbeefUL; }
+            }
+
+            fprintf(F, "%08x refs=%u ptr=%08x d08=%08x d10=%08x d18=%08x d20=%08x "
+                       "d28=%08x d30=%08x d38=%08x d40=%08x\n",
+                    (unsigned)Key, (unsigned)Refs, (unsigned)(size_t)Ptr,
+                    (unsigned)D[0], (unsigned)D[1], (unsigned)D[2], (unsigned)D[3],
+                    (unsigned)D[4], (unsigned)D[5], (unsigned)D[6], (unsigned)D[7]);
+            Hits++;
+        }
+    }
+
+    fclose(F);
+    eprintf("legacy-cache: %d entries -> %s\n", Hits, Path);
+}
+
+static void LegacyScanImageStruct(void)
+{
+    static int Done = 0;
+    unsigned long *Slot;
+    int Hits = 0;
+
+    if (Done || getenv("LEGACY_FIND_IMAGE") == NULL) return;
+    if (SDL_GetTicks() < 25000) return;
+
+    Done = 1;
+
+    for (Slot = (unsigned long *)0x46C000UL; Slot < (unsigned long *)0x4AAFB8UL; Slot++)
+    {
+        unsigned long V = *Slot;
+        unsigned long Key, Type, Sect, Delta;
+
+        if ((V < 0x00100000UL) || (V > 0x7fffffffUL)) continue;
+
+        __try
+        {
+            Key = *(unsigned long *)V;
+            Type = *(unsigned long *)(V + 4);
+            Sect = *(unsigned long *)(V + 0x18);
+        }
+        __except (1)
+        {
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        if ((Key < 0x01000000UL) || (Key > 0x0fffffffUL)) continue;
+        if ((Type > 8) && (Type != 0xffffffffUL)) continue;
+
+        Delta = Sect - V;
+
+        {
+            int k;
+            unsigned long D[8];
+            const int Offs[8] = { 0x08, 0x10, 0x18, 0x20, 0x28, 0x30, 0x38, 0x40 };
+
+            for (k = 0; k < 8; k++)
+            {
+                __try { D[k] = *(unsigned long *)(V + Offs[k]) - V; }
+                __except (1) { D[k] = 0xdeadbeefUL; }
+            }
+
+            if (!((D[0] > 0) && (D[0] < 0x10000UL) && (D[1] > D[0]) &&
+                  (D[3] > D[1]) && (D[4] > D[3]) && (D[4] < 0x2000000UL)))
+            {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            eprintf("legacy-image-scan: key=%08x type=%08x "
+                    "d08=%08x d10=%08x d18=%08x d20=%08x d28=%08x d30=%08x d38=%08x d40=%08x\n",
+                    (unsigned)Key, (unsigned)Type,
+                    (unsigned)D[0], (unsigned)D[1], (unsigned)D[2], (unsigned)D[3],
+                    (unsigned)D[4], (unsigned)D[5], (unsigned)D[6], (unsigned)D[7]);
+        }
+
+        if (++Hits >= 12) break;
+    }
+
+    if (Hits == 0) eprintf("legacy-image-scan: no candidate found\n");
+}
+
+static void LegacyDumpImage(void)
+{
+    static int Done = 0;
+    const char *Dir = getenv("LEGACY_IMAGE_DIR");
+    unsigned long *Slot;
+    unsigned char *Base = NULL;
+    FILE *F;
+    char Path[512];
+
+    if (Done || (Dir == NULL)) return;
+    if (SDL_GetTicks() < 25000) return;
+
+    Done = 1;
+
+    for (Slot = (unsigned long *)0x46C000UL; Slot < (unsigned long *)0x4AAFB8UL; Slot++)
+    {
+        unsigned long V = *Slot;
+        unsigned long Key;
+
+        if ((V < 0x00100000UL) || (V > 0x7fffffffUL)) continue;
+
+        __try { Key = *(unsigned long *)V; }
+        __except (1) { continue; }
+
+        if (Key == 0x01001f41UL)
+        {
+            Base = (unsigned char *)V;
+            eprintf("legacy-image: key 1001f41 live at %p (slot %p)\n", (void *)V, (void *)Slot);
+            break;
+        }
+    }
+
+    if (Base == NULL)
+    {
+        eprintf("legacy-image: key 1001f41 not found\n");
+        return;
+    }
+
+    {
+        int Pass;
+
+        for (Pass = 0; Pass < 2; Pass++)
+        {
+            unsigned char *From;
+            size_t Want = (Pass == 0) ? 0x2000 : 0x4000;
+
+            if (Pass == 0)
+            {
+                From = Base;
+            }
+            else
+            {
+                __try { From = (unsigned char *)*(unsigned long *)(Base + 0x18); }
+                __except (1) { continue; }
+            }
+
+            if (From == NULL) continue;
+
+            snprintf(Path, sizeof(Path), "%s/live_%s.bin", Dir, Pass == 0 ? "struct" : "sect18");
+            F = fopen(Path, "wb");
+
+            if (F != NULL)
+            {
+                size_t Got = 0;
+
+                while (Got < Want)
+                {
+                    unsigned char Chunk[512];
+                    size_t N = Want - Got;
+
+                    if (N > sizeof(Chunk)) N = sizeof(Chunk);
+
+                    __try { memcpy(Chunk, From + Got, N); }
+                    __except (1) { break; }
+
+                    fwrite(Chunk, 1, N, F);
+                    Got += N;
+                }
+
+                fclose(F);
+                eprintf("legacy-image: wrote %s (%u bytes from %p)\n", Path, (unsigned)Got, From);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+
+/* ===========================================================================
+ * Legacy blitter hook - intercept the game's sprite blitter.
+ *
+ * The blitter FUN_00404b20 (original VA, compiled to 0x00404870 in the
+ * recompiled binary) copies one source byte to one destination word: there is
+ * no scaling anywhere in the engine. To draw replacement art larger than its
+ * original pixel grid, we must own that function.
+ *
+ * Stage 1 (this code): install a detour, call through to the original via a
+ * trampoline, log the arguments. Output must be pixel-identical to a normal
+ * run; that is the proof the hook is transparent.
+ *
+ *   LEGACY_BLIT_HOOK=1  install and log
+ * ========================================================================= */
+
+/* The shim does not pull in <windows.h>; declare what the hook needs. */
+#define LEGACY_MEM_COMMIT              0x1000
+#define LEGACY_MEM_RESERVE             0x2000
+#define LEGACY_PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE  0x40
+
+extern void *__stdcall VirtualAlloc(void *lpAddress, unsigned int dwSize, unsigned int flAllocationType, unsigned int flProtect);
+extern int __stdcall VirtualProtect(void *lpAddress, unsigned int dwSize, unsigned int flNewProtect, unsigned int *lpflOldProtect);
+extern int __stdcall FlushInstructionCache(void *hProcess, const void *lpBaseAddress, unsigned int dwSize);
+extern void *__stdcall GetCurrentProcess(void);
+
+typedef unsigned char (__cdecl *LegacyBlitFn)(unsigned int *p1, int p2, int p3, int *p4, unsigned short p5);
+
+static LegacyBlitFn LegacyOrigBlit = 0;
+static int LegacyBlitHooked = 0;
+static int LegacyBlitVerbose = 0;
+
+
+/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+ * Scaled replacement art.
+ *
+ * The engine blitter copies one source byte to one destination word, so a
+ * replacement image can only look sharper if it is drawn LARGER than the
+ * original pixel grid. Art is supplied already scaled (SCL1: magic, u16 w,
+ * u16 h, u16 scale, then w*h RGB565 pixels) and stamped over the original
+ * draw at the same origin.
+ *
+ *   LEGACY_SCALE_DIR=<dir>   contains scale.txt: "KEY ENTRY FILE" per line
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+
+typedef struct
+{
+    unsigned int Key;
+    int Entry;
+    int W;
+    int H;
+    int Scale;
+    unsigned short *Pix;
+} LegacyScaleArt_t;
+
+#define LEGACY_SCALE_MAX 32
+
+static LegacyScaleArt_t LegacyScaleArt[LEGACY_SCALE_MAX];
+static int LegacyScaleCount = 0;
+static int LegacyScaleLoaded = 0;
+
+static void LegacyScaleLoad(void)
+{
+    const char *Dir;
+    char Path[512];
+    char KeyText[64];
+    char FileName[256];
+    FILE *Manifest;
+
+    if (LegacyScaleLoaded) return;
+
+    LegacyScaleLoaded = 1;
+
+    Dir = getenv("LEGACY_SCALE_DIR");
+
+    eprintf("scale: load called, dir=%s hook=%s pack=%s\n", Dir ? Dir : "(null)",
+            getenv("LEGACY_BLIT_HOOK") ? getenv("LEGACY_BLIT_HOOK") : "(null)",
+            getenv("LEGACY_PACK_DIR") ? getenv("LEGACY_PACK_DIR") : "(null)");
+
+    if (Dir == NULL) return;
+
+    if (strlen(Dir) > 400) return;
+
+    strcpy(Path, Dir);
+    strcat(Path, "\\scale.txt");
+
+    Manifest = fopen(Path, "rb");
+
+    if (Manifest == NULL)
+    {
+        eprintf("scale: no manifest at %s\n", Path);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    while ((LegacyScaleCount < LEGACY_SCALE_MAX) &&
+           (fscanf(Manifest, "%63s %d %255s", KeyText, &LegacyScaleArt[LegacyScaleCount].Entry, FileName) == 3))
+    {
+        char Full[600];
+        unsigned char Head[10];
+        FILE *Src;
+        int W, H, Scale, Pixels;
+
+        strcpy(Full, Dir);
+        strcat(Full, "\\");
+        strcat(Full, FileName);
+
+        Src = fopen(Full, "rb");
+
+        if (Src == NULL)
+        {
+            eprintf("scale: cannot open %s\n", Full);
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        if ((fread(Head, 1, 10, Src) != 10) || (memcmp(Head, "SCL1", 4) != 0))
+        {
+            eprintf("scale: bad header %s\n", Full);
+            fclose(Src);
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        W = Head[4] | (Head[5] << 8);
+        H = Head[6] | (Head[7] << 8);
+        Scale = Head[8] | (Head[9] << 8);
+
+        if ((W <= 0) || (H <= 0) || (Scale < 1) || (W > 1024) || (H > 1024))
+        {
+            eprintf("scale: implausible geometry %dx%d x%d in %s\n", W, H, Scale, Full);
+            fclose(Src);
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        Pixels = W * H;
+
+        LegacyScaleArt[LegacyScaleCount].Pix = (unsigned short *)malloc(Pixels * 2);
+
+        if (LegacyScaleArt[LegacyScaleCount].Pix == NULL)
+        {
+            fclose(Src);
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        if ((int)fread(LegacyScaleArt[LegacyScaleCount].Pix, 2, Pixels, Src) != Pixels)
+        {
+            eprintf("scale: short read %s\n", Full);
+            free(LegacyScaleArt[LegacyScaleCount].Pix);
+            fclose(Src);
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        fclose(Src);
+
+        LegacyScaleArt[LegacyScaleCount].Key = (unsigned int)strtoul(KeyText, NULL, 16);
+        LegacyScaleArt[LegacyScaleCount].W = W;
+        LegacyScaleArt[LegacyScaleCount].H = H;
+        LegacyScaleArt[LegacyScaleCount].Scale = Scale;
+
+        eprintf("scale: key %08X entry %d -> %dx%d x%d (%s)\n",
+                LegacyScaleArt[LegacyScaleCount].Key,
+                LegacyScaleArt[LegacyScaleCount].Entry, W, H, Scale, FileName);
+
+        LegacyScaleCount++;
+    }
+
+    fclose(Manifest);
+}
+
+static void LegacyScaleDraw(unsigned short *Base, int Stride, int OriginIndex, const LegacyScaleArt_t *Art, int DestW, int DestH)
+{
+    int x, y, dy, dx, s = Art->Scale;
+
+    for (y = 0; y < Art->H; y++)
+    {
+        for (x = 0; x < Art->W; x++)
+        {
+            unsigned short Colour = Art->Pix[y * Art->W + x];
+
+            if (Colour == 0) continue;
+
+            for (dy = 0; dy < s; dy++)
+            {
+                int Row = OriginIndex + (y * s + dy) * Stride;
+
+                if ((y * s + dy) + (OriginIndex / Stride) >= DestH) break;
+
+                for (dx = 0; dx < s; dx++)
+                {
+                    int Col = x * s + dx;
+
+                    if (Col + (OriginIndex % Stride) >= DestW) break;
+
+                    Base[Row + Col] = Colour;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+/* Draws requested during the frame, painted at present time so nothing
+ * the game blits later can cover them. */
+
+typedef struct
+{
+    int OriginIndex;
+    int Art;
+} LegacyScalePending_t;
+
+#define LEGACY_SCALE_PENDING 16
+
+static LegacyScalePending_t LegacyScalePending[LEGACY_SCALE_PENDING];
+static int LegacyScalePendingCount = 0;
+
+static void LegacyScaleQueue(int OriginIndex, int Art)
+{
+    if (LegacyScalePendingCount >= LEGACY_SCALE_PENDING) return;
+
+    LegacyScalePending[LegacyScalePendingCount].OriginIndex = OriginIndex;
+    LegacyScalePending[LegacyScalePendingCount].Art = Art;
+    LegacyScalePendingCount++;
+}
+
+static void LegacyScaleFlush(unsigned short *Base, int Stride, int DestW, int DestH)
+{
+    static int Flushes = 0;
+    int i;
+
+    Flushes++;
+
+    if ((Flushes % 120) == 1)
+    {
+        eprintf("scale-flush: #%d queued=%d surface=%dx%d stride=%d\n",
+                Flushes, LegacyScalePendingCount, DestW, DestH, Stride);
+    }
+
+    for (i = 0; i < LegacyScalePendingCount; i++)
+    {
+        LegacyScaleDraw(Base, Stride, LegacyScalePending[i].OriginIndex,
+                        &LegacyScaleArt[LegacyScalePending[i].Art], DestW, DestH);
+    }
+
+    LegacyScalePendingCount = 0;
+}
+
+static unsigned char __cdecl LegacyBlitHook(unsigned int *p1, int p2, int p3, int *p4, unsigned short p5)
+{
+    static int Calls = 0;
+    static unsigned int LastKey = 0;
+    static int LastEntry = -1;
+
+    Calls++;
+
+    if (LegacyBlitVerbose)
+    {
+        /* p1[0] is &image[0x18] (see FUN_00409050, which builds the five pointers
+         * on the stack from &local_18), so the image base is p1[0] - 0x18 and the
+         * loader stamps the record key at its offset 0. */
+        unsigned int Img = (unsigned int)p1[0] - 0x18;
+        unsigned int Key = *(unsigned int *)Img;
+        int Entry = *(unsigned short *)((unsigned char *)p3 + 4);
+        int Frame = *(unsigned short *)((unsigned char *)p3 + 2);
+
+        if (((Key >> 24) >= 0x01) && ((Key >> 24) <= 0x7F))
+        {
+            if ((Key != LastKey) || (Entry != LastEntry))
+            {
+                eprintf("blit-key: key=%08X entry=%d frame=%d rect=%d,%d %dx%d\n",
+                        Key, Entry, Frame, p4[0], p4[1], p4[2], p4[3]);
+                LastKey = Key;
+                LastEntry = Entry;
+            }
+        }
+        else if ((Calls % 600) == 1)
+        {
+            eprintf("blit-hook: call#%d image=%08X (no key) rect=%d,%d %dx%d\n",
+                    Calls, Img, p4[0], p4[1], p4[2], p4[3]);
+        }
+    }
+
+    {
+        unsigned char Result = LegacyOrigBlit(p1, p2, p3, p4, p5);
+
+        if (LegacyScaleCount > 0)
+        {
+            unsigned int Img = (unsigned int)p1[0] - 0x18;
+            unsigned int Key2 = *(unsigned int *)Img;
+            int Entry2 = *(unsigned short *)((unsigned char *)p3 + 4);
+            int i;
+
+            for (i = 0; i < LegacyScaleCount; i++)
+            {
+                if ((LegacyScaleArt[i].Key == Key2) && (LegacyScaleArt[i].Entry == Entry2))
+                {
+                    int OriginX = (int)*(unsigned short *)((unsigned char *)p3 + 0x0C);
+                    int OriginY = (int)*(short *)((unsigned char *)p3 + 0x06);
+
+                    eprintf("scale-match: key=%08X entry=%d destbase=%08X p3=%08X "
+                            "f06=%d f08=%d f0a=%d f0c=%d f0e=%d f10=%d origin=%d\n",
+                            Key2, Entry2, (unsigned int)p2, (unsigned int)p3,
+                            (int)*(short *)((unsigned char *)p3 + 0x06),
+                            (int)*(short *)((unsigned char *)p3 + 0x08),
+                            (int)*(unsigned short *)((unsigned char *)p3 + 0x0A),
+                            (int)*(unsigned short *)((unsigned char *)p3 + 0x0C),
+                            (int)*(unsigned short *)((unsigned char *)p3 + 0x0E),
+                            (int)*(unsigned short *)((unsigned char *)p3 + 0x10),
+                            OriginX * 640 + OriginY);
+
+                    LegacyScaleQueue(OriginX * 640 + OriginY, i);
+                    break;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        return Result;
+    }
+}
+
+static void LegacyBlitHookInstall(void)
+{
+    unsigned char *Target = (unsigned char *)0x00404870;
+    unsigned char *Tramp;
+    unsigned int Old;
+    const char *Env;
+
+    if (LegacyBlitHooked) return;
+
+    Env = getenv("LEGACY_BLIT_HOOK");
+    if ((Env == NULL) || (Env[0] == '0') || (Env[0] == 0)) return;
+
+    LegacyBlitVerbose = 1;
+
+    if (!((Target[0] == 0x83) && (Target[1] == 0xEC) && (Target[2] == 0x3C)))
+    {
+        eprintf("blit-hook: prologue mismatch %02X %02X %02X\n", Target[0], Target[1], Target[2]);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    Tramp = (unsigned char *)VirtualAlloc(0, 64, LEGACY_MEM_COMMIT | LEGACY_MEM_RESERVE, LEGACY_PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE);
+
+    if (Tramp == 0)
+    {
+        eprintf("blit-hook: VirtualAlloc failed\n");
+        return;
+    }
+
+    memcpy(Tramp, Target, 10);                       /* sub esp,0x3c ; cmpl $0,0x486078 */
+    Tramp[10] = 0xE9;
+    *(int *)(Tramp + 11) = (int)(Target + 10) - (int)(Tramp + 15);
+
+    if (VirtualProtect(Target, 16, LEGACY_PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE, &Old))
+    {
+        Target[0] = 0xE9;
+        *(int *)(Target + 1) = (int)&LegacyBlitHook - (int)(Target + 5);
+
+        Target[5] = 0x90; Target[6] = 0x90; Target[7] = 0x90;
+        Target[8] = 0x90; Target[9] = 0x90;
+
+        VirtualProtect(Target, 16, Old, &Old);
+        FlushInstructionCache(GetCurrentProcess(), Target, 16);
+    }
+
+    LegacyOrigBlit = (LegacyBlitFn)Tramp;
+    LegacyBlitHooked = 1;
+
+    LegacyScaleLoad();
+
+    eprintf("blit-hook: installed at %08X, trampoline %08X\n", (unsigned int)Target, (unsigned int)Tramp);
+}
+
+static void LegacyBlitMark(struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *Dst, struct _rect *DstRect,
+                           struct IDirectDrawSurface_c *Src, struct _rect *SrcRect)
+{
+    FILE *Log = LegacyBlitLogFile();
+
+    LegacyArtOverride(Src);
+    LegacyDumpSurface(Src);
+    LegacyDumpImage();
+
+    LegacyBlitCount++;
+
+    if ((Log == NULL) || (LegacyBlitCount > 300))
+    {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    fprintf(Log, "blit dst=%dx%d dstrect=(%d,%d,%d,%d) src=%dx%d srcrect=(%d,%d,%d,%d)\n",
+            LegacySurfW(Dst), LegacySurfH(Dst),
+            DstRect ? DstRect->left : -1, DstRect ? DstRect->top : -1,
+            DstRect ? DstRect->right : -1, DstRect ? DstRect->bottom : -1,
+            LegacySurfW(Src), LegacySurfH(Src),
+            SrcRect ? SrcRect->left : -1, SrcRect ? SrcRect->top : -1,
+            SrcRect ? SrcRect->right : -1, SrcRect ? SrcRect->bottom : -1);
+}
+
+static void LegacyLockMark(void)
+{
+    LegacyLockCount++;
+}
+
+static void LegacyInputPump(void);
+
+static void LegacyFrameEnd(void)
+{
+    LegacyInputPump();
+    FILE *Log = LegacyBlitLogFile();
+
+    if (Log == NULL)
+    {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    LegacyFrameNo++;
+    fprintf(Log, "frame %lu blits=%lu locks=%lu\n", LegacyFrameNo, LegacyBlitCount, LegacyLockCount);
+    fflush(Log);
+
+    LegacyBlitCount = 0;
+    LegacyLockCount = 0;
+}
+/* ---- legacy scripted input ------------------------------------------------
+ * LEGACY_INPUT="6000,click,120,450;9000,key,13" replays actions at the given
+ * millisecond offsets so a windowless run can walk the menus.
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+static void LegacyInputPump(void)
+{
+    static int State = 0;
+    static unsigned long DueMs[32];
+    static char Kind[32][8];
+    static int ArgA[32];
+    static int ArgB[32];
+    static unsigned char Done[32];
+    static int Count = 0;
+    static Uint32 Start = 0;
+
+    if (State == 0)
+    {
+        const char *Env = getenv("LEGACY_INPUT");
+
+        State = -1;
+
+        if ((Env != NULL) && (Env[0] != '\0'))
+        {
+            char Buffer[1024];
+            char *Entry;
+
+            snprintf(Buffer, sizeof(Buffer), "%s", Env);
+            Entry = Buffer;
+
+            while ((Entry != NULL) && (*Entry != '\0') && (Count < 32))
+            {
+                char *Next = strchr(Entry, ';');
+                unsigned long Ms = 0;
+                char KindBuf[8] = "";
+                int A = 0;
+                int B = 0;
+
+                if (Next != NULL)
+                {
+                    *Next = '\0';
+                    Next++;
+                }
+
+                if (sscanf(Entry, "%lu,%7[^,],%d,%d", &Ms, KindBuf, &A, &B) >= 3)
+                {
+                    DueMs[Count] = Ms;
+                    snprintf(Kind[Count], sizeof(Kind[Count]), "%s", KindBuf);
+                    ArgA[Count] = A;
+                    ArgB[Count] = B;
+                    Done[Count] = 0;
+                    Count++;
+                }
+
+                Entry = Next;
+            }
+
+            if (Count > 0)
+            {
+                State = 1;
+                eprintf("legacy-input: %d actions scheduled\n", Count);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    if (State != 1) return;
+    if (Start == 0) Start = SDL_GetTicks();
+
+    {
+        Uint32 Now = SDL_GetTicks() - Start;
+        int i;
+
+        for (i = 0; i < Count; i++)
+        {
+            if ((Done[i] == 0) && (Now >= (Uint32)DueMs[i]))
+            {
+                Done[i] = 1;
+
+                if (strcmp(Kind[i], "key") == 0)
+                {
+                    Patch_PushKeyEvent((uint32_t)ArgA[i]);
+                    eprintf("legacy-input: t=%lu key %d\n", (unsigned long)Now, ArgA[i]);
+                }
+                else if (strcmp(Kind[i], "click") == 0)
+                {
+                    Patch_PushMouseClick(ArgA[i], ArgB[i]);
+                    eprintf("legacy-input: t=%lu click %d,%d\n", (unsigned long)Now, ArgA[i], ArgB[i]);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+
+/* ---- legacy frame capture -------------------------------------------------
+ * Gives a windowless run eyes: LEGACY_SHOT_DIR=<dir> saves a BMP every
+ * LEGACY_SHOT_EVERY presents (default 60). Used to verify rendering and art
+ * packs without a desktop session.
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+static void LegacyScreenshot(SDL_Renderer *Renderer)
+{
+    static char Dir[256];
+    static int State = 0;
+    static unsigned long Calls = 0;
+    static unsigned long Every = 60;
+    SDL_Surface *Shot;
+    int Width, Height;
+    char Path[512];
+
+    if (State == 0)
+    {
+        const char *EnvDir = getenv("LEGACY_SHOT_DIR");
+        const char *EnvEvery = getenv("LEGACY_SHOT_EVERY");
+
+        State = -1;
+
+        if ((EnvDir != NULL) && (EnvDir[0] != '\0'))
+        {
+            snprintf(Dir, sizeof(Dir), "%s", EnvDir);
+            if (EnvEvery != NULL)
+            {
+                unsigned long Value = (unsigned long)strtoul(EnvEvery, NULL, 10);
+                if (Value > 0) Every = Value;
+            }
+            State = 1;
+            eprintf("legacy-shot: enabled dir=%s every=%lu\n", Dir, Every);
+        }
+    }
+
+    if ((State != 1) || (Renderer == NULL)) return;
+
+    Calls++;
+    if ((Calls % Every) != 0) return;
+
+    if (SDL_GetRendererOutputSize(Renderer, &Width, &Height) != 0) return;
+
+    Shot = SDL_CreateRGBSurfaceWithFormat(0, Width, Height, 32, SDL_PIXELFORMAT_ARGB8888);
+    if (Shot == NULL) return;
+
+    if (SDL_RenderReadPixels(Renderer, NULL, SDL_PIXELFORMAT_ARGB8888, Shot->pixels, Shot->pitch) == 0)
+    {
+        snprintf(Path, sizeof(Path), "%s/shot_%05lu.bmp", Dir, Calls);
+        SDL_SaveBMP(Shot, Path);
+    }
+
+    SDL_FreeSurface(Shot);
+}

 {

             // clear next frame
             SDL_SetRenderDrawColor(lpThis->Renderer, 0, 0, 0, 255);

             SDL_RenderPresent(lpThis->Renderer);

+/* ---- legacy post-process upscale (EPX / Scale2x) --------------------------
+ * The engine renders 640x480 no matter what. This takes that finished frame and
+ * doubles it with edge-aware interpolation instead of blocky nearest-neighbour.
+ * Wrapper only: no engine code involved.
+ *   LEGACY_UPSCALE=2x   enable
+ *   (unset / anything else) off, byte-for-byte original behaviour
+ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+static int LegacyUpscaleFactor(void)
+{
+    static int Factor = 0;
+
+    if (Factor == 0)
+    {
+        const char *Env = getenv("LEGACY_UPSCALE");
+
+        Factor = 1;
+
+        if ((Env != NULL) &&
+            ((strcmp(Env, "2x") == 0) || (strcmp(Env, "epx") == 0) || (strcmp(Env, "2") == 0)))
+        {
+            Factor = 2;
+            eprintf("legacy-upscale: EPX 2x enabled\n");
+        }
+    }
+
+    return Factor;
+}
+
+static uint32_t LegacyReadPixel(const uint8_t *p, int BytesPerPixel)
+{
+    if (BytesPerPixel == 1) return *p;
+    if (BytesPerPixel == 2) return *(const uint16_t *)p;
+    return *(const uint32_t *)p;
+}
+
+static void LegacyWritePixel(uint8_t *p, int BytesPerPixel, uint32_t Value)
+{
+    if (BytesPerPixel == 1) { *p = (uint8_t)Value; }
+    else if (BytesPerPixel == 2) { *(uint16_t *)p = (uint16_t)Value; }
+    else { *(uint32_t *)p = Value; }
+}
+
+static void LegacyUpscaleEPX2(const uint8_t *Src, int Width, int Height, int SrcPitch,
+                              uint8_t *Dst, int DstPitch, int BytesPerPixel)
+{
+    int x, y;
+
+    for (y = 0; y < Height; y++)
+    {
+        const uint8_t *Row = Src + (size_t)y * SrcPitch;
+        const uint8_t *UpRow = Src + (size_t)((y > 0) ? (y - 1) : y) * SrcPitch;
+        const uint8_t *DownRow = Src + (size_t)((y < Height - 1) ? (y + 1) : y) * SrcPitch;
+        uint8_t *OutTop = Dst + (size_t)(2 * y) * DstPitch;
+        uint8_t *OutBottom = OutTop + DstPitch;
+
+        for (x = 0; x < Width; x++)
+        {
+            int Left = (x > 0) ? (x - 1) : x;
+            int Right = (x < Width - 1) ? (x + 1) : x;
+            uint32_t P = LegacyReadPixel(Row + (size_t)x * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel);
+            uint32_t A = LegacyReadPixel(UpRow + (size_t)x * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel);
+            uint32_t D = LegacyReadPixel(DownRow + (size_t)x * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel);
+            uint32_t C = LegacyReadPixel(Row + (size_t)Left * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel);
+            uint32_t B = LegacyReadPixel(Row + (size_t)Right * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel);
+            uint32_t E0 = P, E1 = P, E2 = P, E3 = P;
+
+            if ((C == A) && (C != D) && (A != B)) E0 = A;
+            if ((A == B) && (A != C) && (B != D)) E1 = B;
+            if ((D == C) && (D != B) && (C != A)) E2 = C;
+            if ((B == D) && (B != A) && (D != C)) E3 = D;
+
+            LegacyWritePixel(OutTop + (size_t)(2 * x) * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel, E0);
+            LegacyWritePixel(OutTop + (size_t)(2 * x + 1) * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel, E1);
+            LegacyWritePixel(OutBottom + (size_t)(2 * x) * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel, E2);
+            LegacyWritePixel(OutBottom + (size_t)(2 * x + 1) * BytesPerPixel, BytesPerPixel, E3);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
